## Established facts, Volume 3 complete (ch 119–178) — cold-reader standing record

**Volume 3 is closed and audited.** Sixty chapters, **135,014 words** (Batch 0001 36,809 · Batch 0002 36,742 ·
Batch 0003 30,958 · Batch 0004 30,505), average ~2,250. The volume ran **day 2 to day 219**. The audit
table, all thirteen items and every repair is in `state/current.md` under `## Volume 3 close audit —
CLOSED`; **the close review's reversal of the footprint rule supersedes it wherever the two disagree,
and is written up under *Close review repair* in that same block.**

This block consolidates the whole volume in one place for a model with no memory of it. Nothing below
is a plan. Everything is on the page.

- **The relicensed licence (ch 130–132).** Granted by **Sun He**, Registrar, at the Lowwater county
  office on the square. **Clause Four, ceiling at Condensing nine in her own hand.** The defect
  re-entered in her own hand under a new sentence she wrote herself: *it restores nothing.* **Seven
  conditions on the back — read once at ch 132 and referenced, never re-listed, anywhere in ch 119–178.**
  **The eight hundred jin is the seventh and the last thing on the paper.** Relicensing restored
  nothing: not the aperture, not the fourth stage, not a surety, not the road.
- **The three legs, and their order.** **Clearance (ch 130, Shen Xiu's second clearance) → the three
  sponsors' seal on one sheet (ch 131, Lu Kang, Deng Chao, Pei An) → Sun He's countersignature
  (ch 132).** The order is **inverted from Volume 2 on purpose** and must not be tidied back. Motive:
  the sponsors' annex figure has to be level before three men will seal a ceiling above five.
  Hours record continuous from the same origin date as the first — the day the tithe book opened.
- **The claim as the seventh and last condition.** Eight hundred jin, **standing, unanswered**, travelling
  on the licence. **Hou Sanliang unpaid, patient, and never in a room with her, never met.** The ch 150
  handover would have paid him in full out of the Warden's purse and **she refused it aloud before two
  witnesses and signed the refusal.** **A date has been entered against the claim in Hou's counting
  room in Lowwater, by a hand that is not the counting room's.** A date against a claim is a schedule.
  Not resolved. Not tore off.
- **Four stages in the volume, and the three holes.** **Condensing 5 → 6 (ch 135, announced) · 6 → 7
  (ch 148, announced) · 7 → 8 (ch 160, SUPPRESSED at her order, no bracketed line in the chapter at all)
  · 8 → 9 (ch 171, ANNOUNCED at her order, witnessed by nine men at the cold end).** **Four bracketed
  layer lines in sixty chapters** — 135, 140 (a Merit bracket, not a stage), 148, 171. **No tribulation
  anywhere. No skipped breakthrough.**
  **The stage column reads one, two, three, five, six, seven, nine — three holes in nine lines.**
  **NINE counties is what is HEARD; FOUR counties is what is WRITTEN** (V1 ch 46, *in four counties of
  nine*; ch 55, *going round nine counties… and Du Hong had read her four counties' versions*). **The
  column is entered in the county books of four counties out of nine**; a certified copy is *readable
  in order by anybody in nine counties with a stamp and a reason* (ch 132) — that is obtainability,
  not the location of a book. **The close audit wrote this backwards; the close review reversed it and
  ch 160, 163 and 176 are back at four.**
- **Yuan's stage and the clock.** **Voice throughout.** Ledger held **once** in the volume, at **ch 149**,
  on her spoken word, in public on the copying floor, **under a day, at a price of four and a half weeks
  named aloud before the word**, put down the same day and **refused permanently for the volume**.
  **From ch 150 Ledger is hers to order and his to hold only on her word — and at ch 178 she has it to
  order and does not order it.** Standing Voice terms: he sees her arm go before she can, at no cost,
  and names the price before he spends it. **Channel at ch 178: about ten days**, said once, in weeks,
  in the final image. **Merit: one listed kill (ch 140, on the escort road), told-before-spent, and
  nothing else in sixty chapters.**
- **The audit and the Lung, and what they cost Feng.** Audit **ch 136–140**, offer **ch 141**, taken
  **ch 143**. He answered the lawful request truly and could not answer it partly. **A quarter off the
  stair, a district clerk carrying the upward bundle, the four walled county copies read at the county
  table, his fair-hand line re-entered truly upward.** He is **not dismissed**: volumes, stipend, column
  and house stay, and *the district does not terminate an examination.* **Column at five. A Listener for
  life — wage, oil, a hundredweight of coal, a chair in the county office on reading days — for
  reporting the stair upward truly in full.** Warm for the first time in twenty-two years, coughing less,
  writing more, sixty-two, owned. **Has not asked her a question since ch 143. Not thanked. The second
  Lung is Volume 11 and is unspent.**
- **The milestone (ch 150).** She chose to be **hunted** rather than **handed over** (series §7 item 4).
  A true mercy, **verified true line by line** through her own eyes at the cost of four and a half
  weeks she named before the word, refused aloud before two witnesses in the county office on the
  square, and **signed in her own hand.** The paper carried **no agent's name**; a clerk taking the
  name of the agent who laid it is how **Bai Zhu** is named, **once, at ch 150, to a form.** The refusal
  is entered in a Warden's book, on her member's sheet, and in the county book. **Nothing in ch 119–178
  softens, defers, reopens or regrets it**, no chapter lets her wonder what she would have done, and
  at **ch 174** she checks, standing in the room, that she does not wish she had said yes.
- **The petition (ch 175).** Four lines on Hall paper, her filing copy of the licence folded in behind
  them **front outward** so the seventh condition went up the stair with it. **Received, numbered,
  routed, BARRED — not refused — and not withdrawable by her.** Ground of the bar: **this county holds
  no schedule of work done past its boundary and cannot certify one** — and expressly not the standing
  of the applicant. Last line, written slower than the rest: *the answer to this application is not this
  office's to write.* Dated the day after a referral went up out of this county; both papers rode the
  same crate.
- **The referral (ch 174).** **Read twice in a low room in front of twenty people.** **Bai Zhu certified
  in a Warden's hand, signed by the office and not by a person,** that the System is real, unauthorised
  and carried by a licensee in good standing whose seventh condition travels with her; and **said out
  loud, once, in his own mouth, that a field man of a Warden's office cannot unmake a System inside the
  bound without a Wall writ, there is no such writ in this county, and he is referring the dismantling
  upward to the Warden.** The referral **carries her name, her stages in order and her hours, carries no
  agent's name, and has gone up the stair past every office she has ever stood in front of.**
- **The district's answer and the empty space (ch 176).** The public shelf by the door carries the
  district's answer to his request: **received, numbered, referred upward, no instrument held in the
  district either — and an empty space the width of a line where an answer was meant to go.**
  **Volume 4 opens on that space and must not fill it in the first batch.**
- **The register, the plate, and the four documents.** **The register still reads *reconciled*** for
  Ning Wu; the truth unchanged. **The Clay Plate is still worn against her ribs**, above the oilcloth,
  and has never been surrendered. **The second licence is nailed STRAIGHT above the second bunk; the
  first is rolled in the lining of her coat,** taken down before it went soft at the crease of a defect
  line she wrote herself.
- **Held objects:** the Clay Plate · the oilcloth and licence against her ribs · **a tin of about a
  hundred and forty coppers under a board in the corner of the wash house where the water does not get**
  · the four county volumes of her hours · the ch 66 refusal under the cover of the tithe book · the
  ch 103 surety transfer reading *Stands to me* · the ch 106 no-surety request.
- **Crew.** **Nine men and her. Ten sleepers in twelve bunks. Never written as ten men.** They have read
  the refusal, the ninth, the word on the front of their own schedule and three holes in a column, and
  **Gao has done the sum out loud in a hall** so that nine men have heard it from a mouth.
  **Qin, twenty-two, walks four miles to stand eight feet off in a queue while she writes a column and
  never asks her a question.** Gao holds the frame.
- **The girl of twenty-four.** **Alive.** Leg set crooked, four paid hours a week at the trough **plus
  four more out of a district fund for the cold months**, because she wrote the second document and not
  the first. Paid a quarter to the thaw in advance. **Not thanked** — she has set not-being-thanked up as
  protection for two volumes. Copying carriers' run lists that nobody has ever wanted.
- **Not on any paper, and not to be resolved in Volume 4's first batch.** ***Kiln-born*** in two heads
  and no paper; the volumes on the high shelf are not copies and were not struck. **The office name
  above this county, which Yuan cannot read and has now said four times he cannot and is not going to
  say it a fifth time to be different about it.** **What became of the eleven.** The design of the
  Foundation tax. Whether Ning Wu forgives her. The Foundation writ, dead and not revoked.
- **Retired numbers and rhythms.** **"Eleven weeks" is retired out of the volume** — derive the
  Hunter's span from **day 2** and the end of winter from **about day 261**. **"About four seconds" is a
  rhythm and not a rule** (thirty-six uses across twenty-six chapters); if a pause needs a number, use a
  different one. **No weekday is named anywhere in ch 119–178.**

### Volume 4 material (decided in `outline/volume-04.md`)

The relicensing's cost opening the volume — a ceiling at nine buys a ceiling, not a body, and the second
relicensing needs a schedule of hours worked past this county's boundary. The answer to the empty space.
The Wall as a shape of work rather than a shape of stone. Foundation 1 → 9 and what the stages cost.
**Wei Ran may first appear in this volume, twenty, mortal-born** (`outline/series.md` §7; she is absent
from Volumes 1–3 and the line goes off page and is not the main relationship).

### Planned in `outline/batches/volume-04-batch-0001.md` — Batch 0001, ch 179–192, **NOW DRAFTED**

**All fourteen chapters are on the page.** What follows was the plan and is kept because Batch 0002's
drafter reads continuity before it reads the cards. **Where the draft and the plan differ, the draft is
recorded in the block above and the one difference that matters is:** the plan put the tin's projection at
day 246 and the gap before the road at eight days; the draft carries the arithmetic from the county's own
month table, which gives **the end of this month and seven days**. Same shape, one day out, and **seven days
is the figure on the page.** Everything else in the plan was drafted as written.

- **The fourth monthly attendance is day 239, and she attends it.** Month-day = `((day − 134) mod 30) + 1` on a
  thirty-day month, and 239 is **the sixteenth**, at the third hour, in person, in her own hand. **Derive it that
  way and not from any leftover count.** (This line once read *239 − 133 = 106, and 106 leaves 16 over three whole
  months*; the subtraction and the 106 were wrong and the month-day is the sixteenth directly.)
- **Why thirty days and not the thirty-one the first three ran at — a drift that ends here, recorded so
  nobody "corrects" it.** The three attendances Volume 3 put on the page are **day 147, day 178 and day
  209**, thirty-one days apart, which on this formula walks them **the fourteenth, the fifteenth and the
  sixteenth.** Ch 168 states the standing order in Sun He's own entry as *the attendance was on the
  sixteenth* — *three days before the reading* — and **209 is the sixteenth**, so **the day of the month
  does not move and the gap is thirty: 209 + 30 = 239.** **A draft read day 240 and the seventeenth; that
  was wrong and is retired.** **The Volume 3 seam still stands** — no closed chapter states the gap
  between day 147 and day 178 and none may be given one — **and the thirty days from 209 to 239 is the
  first such gap a chapter may state.** **Missing the attendance is Batch 0002's irreversible decision at
  ch 205 and must not be planted in Batch 0001.**
- **The tin is never empty in Batch 0001.** About a hundred and forty coppers at ch 178 (day 219) at four
  loads a week and nine coppers a load; the projection runs out on **about day 246**, eight days short of
  a road that opens on about day 254; and **she stops buying on day 240, in daylight, and holds what is
  left — about thirty coppers — for the road.** From the 240th the tin is **about thirty coppers under a
  board she is choosing not to spend**, and **no card after ch 190 and no hand-off line may call it
  empty, spent or gone.** A draft carried *a tin with nothing in it* into the ch 191 pull, the ch 192
  inventory and the Batch 0002 hand-off; that coinage is retired.

## Established facts, Volume 4 Batch 0001 (ch 179–192) — RATION AND SILENCE

**Fourteen chapters, 28,608 words, average ~2,043.** Day **220 to day 253**, and nothing falls between
batches: **Batch 0002 opens on day 254** with the season's road open. **Zero bracketed layer lines in
fourteen chapters.** The whole batch is a ration-and-silence pressure and none of Volume 3's four shapes
and none of Volume 2's confinement is rebuilt in it.

### The clock, as drafted

- **Day 0 is the last day of the thaw. Ch 178 is day 219; ch 179 is day 220; ch 192 ends on day 253; ch 193 is day 254.**
- **Month-day = `((day − 134) mod 30) + 1` on a thirty-day month.** The fourth monthly attendance is **day 239, the sixteenth**,
  at the third hour, **thirty days** on from the third at day 209 — the first gap between two attendances a
  chapter has been allowed to state. **She attends it.** Missing it is Batch 0002's irreversible decision at
  ch 205 and **nothing in Batch 0001 plants it, foreshadows it or lets her think about it.**
- **The season's road opens on the eighth of the ninth month, or later, and not earlier** — the county
  notice on the public shelf, sealed, with a thumbprint of melted and refrozen frost on the corner. The thaw
  falls on about the same day: at day 243 it is **eleven days off**.
- **The tin.** About a hundred and forty coppers at ch 178 (day 219). Four loads a week at nine coppers =
  thirty-six a week. The projection runs out **at the end of this month** and the road is the eighth of the
  next: **seven days short.** She pays **four coppers** to the girl at ch 183 (a shade under a day of stone,
  so the projection does not move). She **stops buying on the morning of day 240** with about thirty coppers
  left, which is about six nights of stone. **From the 240th the tin is about thirty coppers under a board
  she is choosing not to spend.** **Nothing after ch 190 calls it empty, spent or gone, and no later chapter
  re-counts it against a different figure.**
- **The channel.** *About ten days* was said once at ch 178 and **is not repeated in ch 179–184.** The price
  reaches a page **twice, both times as the price**: ch 185 (**eight days out of about ten**) and ch 187
  (named again immediately before the word). **What is left, about two days, is said once at ch 187 and she
  refuses to have it said twice.** The ration is **four a day** (ch 184, in her own hand on the card, and
  referred to after that, never restated and never multiplied by a number of days), and **ch 191 is the only
  place the falling-off goes into numbers — three, then two, then one.** Ch 192 states what is left of him
  **once, in her own count, with no figure in it.** No bare Settlement fraction anywhere. No total and no
  arithmetic on any page.
- **Ledger.** Taken **once** (ch 187), price named before the word, held less than a day, put down when it is
  over and **not offered to hold it again**. **Refused once out loud** (ch 191), on a mat, **before he can
  say the price**, with no explanation attached. **Merit: none. Nothing in ch 179–192 can restore the channel.**

### What the batch established on the page

1. **A ruled space on a form is the place where an entry gets made, not an unanswered question** (ch 179).
   The office that fills it is the one the referral went to. **The space is written on the page more than
   once and is never filled** — its first entry is Batch 0003 and its answer is Batch 0004.
2. **The county publishes a date and a clerk has sealed it onto a boundary** (ch 179). She can see the edge
   of the whole of her position from inside it. The county's day and the carriers' word differ by about a
   week (ch 183), and the carriers are the ones who go and look.
3. **A Ledger price is paid out of a quantity of channel and not out of time.** The reading is short; the
   price is what is gone afterwards (ch 187).
4. **A voice can be rationed, and the person rationing him is worse at it than he is**, because he wants to
   spend them and she wants to save them (ch 184, ch 191).
5. **Her eleven minutes and her four miles are worth nothing to nine men** (ch 186). The signed-shift rule is
   *used* — she cannot sign for nine men because she is not one of the crew — and not explained.
6. **The word *irregular* was on the front of the quarterly for the fourth time and is not stamped a fifth**
   (ch 186). The Hall sheet is written out by her in eleven minutes in a hall with no roof.
7. **What the eight days bought (ch 187):** **Luo Ping**, thirty-nine, a schedule-man with sixteen years of
   other people's schedules, **with a wife and a boy of eleven who live past this county's boundary.**
   **What it did not buy:** her, the claim, the date, the reason for any of it, the answer on the shelf.
   **The claim stands and the date stays in the counting room in Lowwater. Hou Sanliang is not met and does
   not appear.**
8. **The year of non-forgiveness changed shape and did not close (ch 185 and ch 188).** The once-a-year
   reserved sentence was spent **on the price**, so that when the year came round there was nothing set
   aside for it. What replaced it is **a purchase and not forgiveness**, plus relief at not having to prove
   to an empty room that she is angry. **The account still carries and must still carry at ch 1000.**
9. **She stopped buying the stone on the morning of day 240 (ch 190)** and made nine men cold for four nights
   on purpose. **Nobody is hurt, nothing breaks, nobody dies, nobody is rescued.** **Qin noticed the fire had
   gone out, filled a bucket himself and asked nothing.**
10. **What a carrier knows about a road is loads, days, and a place where a cart turns** (ch 191), and the
    run lists show that **the carriers do not run past a certain point because there is nothing on the far
    side of it for them to run to.** That is a fact about carriers and **is not a description of anything and
    is not written as one.**

### Construction budget — spent in Batch 0001, not to reuse

Bracketed layer lines: **zero, in fourteen chapters.** The seven relicensing conditions re-listed: **zero**
(referenced once in ch 179 and ch 192 as the back of the licence). The suppression arithmetic: **zero** — at
ch 189 the three gaps are ruled in the ordinary four words and Sun He **does not say the ruling aloud**, which
is the Volume 3 arrangement restated as silence. The signed-shift roster rule re-explained: **zero.** *There
is no form*: **zero. There is no column*: **zero. The record remained*: **zero.** *Nobody asked*: **zero.**
*Nobody knew*: **zero.** *Nobody was thanked*: **one**, at ch 183, and it is the girl — the one place in the
book where that construction is allowed, because she has arranged it in advance for two years and has told
three washerwomen. *Nobody said anything*: **two**, at ch 183 and ch 184, spaced. *Nothing*: kept under
control; the batch is ration-and-silence and the word is used where the subject is the ration. *The line*:
**never used of the Wall.** **No Wall described, crossed or named; no geography past a marker post; the word
*the line* is not available; no office named; no second outside-bound request; no new final enemy.** The four
seconds: **zero.** *Eleven weeks*: **zero.** **Weekdays: zero named anywhere in ch 179–192** — five were
caught in draft (Tuesday, Friday ×2, Thursday) and cut. Banned reflexes: **zero.** Authorial scaffolding:
**zero**; *this chapter*, *this volume*, *the reader*, *the whole series*, and the word *batch*: **zero** —
one leaked into ch 186 and one into ch 190 and both were cut.

### Carried forward, unchanged, into Batch 0002 (ch 193–208)

- **Condensing 9, announced, ceiling nine in her own hand.** **Nothing above nine on the front of the document
  anywhere in the world.** The stage column reads **one, two, three, five, six, seven, nine** — three holes
  in nine lines, **in the county books of four counties out of nine and read out across all nine.**
- **The second relicensing** is petitioned, numbered, received, routed, **barred, not refused**, and **not
  withdrawable by her**; only an office above this county can complete it, and only on a schedule this county
  does not keep. **Foundation 1 is ch 207, in Batch 0002.**
- **The referral** is up the stair past every office she has ever stood in front of, carries her name, her
  stages in order and her hours, **carries no agent's name**, and can only be executed past a boundary no
  county paper crosses. **The district's answer is still four words and an empty space.**
- **The claim stands** — eight hundred jin, seventh and last on the licence — with a **date entered against it
  in Hou Sanliang's counting room in Lowwater by a hand that is not that room's**, by a man now known to be
  **Luo Ping**. **The date was not torn off. Hou is unpaid, patient and never met.**
- **Feng Lushi** alive, warm, column at five, a Listener for life, a quarter off his own stair at his own
  written request, **has not asked her a question since the autumn and is not thanked.** No second appointment
  and no second Lung before Volume 11. **The copying floor and the county office are different rooms.**
- **The girl of twenty-four** alive, a crooked leg, four paid hours a week plus four out of a district fund,
  a quarter paid forward to the thaw, **not thanked**, and now holding forty-one run lists nobody wanted.
- **Nine men and her. Ten sleepers in twelve bunks. Never ten men.** *Irregular* stamped for the fourth time
  and not a fifth. **Qin stands beside her in a queue now. Gao holds the frame and has said the sum out loud
  twice.**
- **Da Shan**: board behind a well, eight frames, nine coppers a load, **has lost four loads a week** and said
  the true thing about it and was not thanked. **He never asks.**
- **Yuan.** Voice. **About two days of channel left**, rationed, thinning. **Ledger is hers to order and his
  to hold only on her word; taken once at ch 187 and refused once at ch 191.** **He still cannot read the
  office name above this county and has not said so a fifth time in this batch.** He is silent about it
  throughout, which is the correct use of him here.
- **The register still reads *reconciled*. The Clay Plate is still worn.** Shen Xiu does not appear. **The
  three sponsors silent** and nothing about their second book is lodged, copied or spoken in ch 179–192.
  **What became of the eleven is not raised.**
- **She does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop.** The ch 150 refusal is not softened,
  deferred, reopened or regretted anywhere in the batch and no chapter lets her wonder what she would have
  done.

### Repaired after the Batch 0001 review — one standing fault and two small ones, now closed

`logs/next-0001.review.log` swept ch 179–192 and found one substantive fault and two minor ones. All three
are repaired. **No beat, figure, name, date or decision was changed, and nothing Batch 0002 owns was
opened or moved.**

- **Present-tense narration on Ning Yan and on other named characters.** `outline/volume-04.md` *Hard rules*
  and `outline/batches/volume-04-batch-0001.md` both set it: *Every sentence Yuan speaks is written in the
  present tense. **No other character is ever in the present tense***. The batch broke this in roughly thirty
  lines across twelve chapters — present perfect (*She has spent*, *she has never been inside it*, *She had
  noticed*) and simple present (*She is going to keep spending*, *She is eighteen years old*, *She is the
  only person in that hall who cannot pay anybody*). **All repaired to past or past perfect.** Worst and
  most load-bearing was **ch 188**, the batch's payoff: the ration block, the relief block and the agreement
  to spend him were **re-voiced by hand, not swapped**, so the landing keeps its beat — *She had noticed.
  She was going to keep spending.* Also repaired: **ch 190**, the block where nine men state what they know
  about her (now *They knew she was…* through four paragraphs, with the *and not one of them had ever asked
  her* anaphora intact); **ch 187**, the Luo Ping dossier (now past, with *He had a wife. He had a boy of
  eleven.* and *He was a man with a family.* unchanged in cadence); and one-line drift in ch 179, 180, 181,
  182, 185, 186, 189 and 191. **The structural present about forms, offices, counties and classes of person
  was left alone and must be left alone** — *A ruled space on a form is not a question*, *a schedule-man
  goes into a counting room*, *there is nowhere in this county she is allowed to keep a book*, *a carrier
  knows which side of a cutting holds snow*. That register is house voice and is not a fault.
- **The ch 187 repeated-number tic.** *Nine minutes' walk* stood twice within six lines, with *about nine
  seconds* in the same sentence. **The second distance and the seconds figure are out.** The geography is
  established once, in *What Came Back*, and the afternoon now turns on *before she had her breath back*.
  Nine minutes is still stated once in ch 187 and once in ch 188, where it is the point of the scene.
- **An overclaim in `workspace/volume-04-batch-0001/SUMMARY.md`**, which certified the tense rule as
  *checked mechanically*. The check had matched neither present-perfect narration nor the POV subject, so it
  missed all thirty lines. **The claim is corrected in place**, with the grep that would actually catch this
  written down for the next batch.
- **A second overclaim the review did not catch**, of the same species and in the same two files:
  *Nothing is under the 1,800 guide* was false of ch 186, at 1,745 before this repair and 1,747 after it.
  **Corrected in `state/book-metadata.md`, in the batch SUMMARY, and here.** The Volume 3 entry in
  `book-metadata.md` already states the convention correctly — naming the chapters that sit outside the
  guide rather than asserting that none do — and Volume 4 now matches it.

**Counts after this repair:** Batch 0001 **28,608** (was 28,611). Longest ch 179 **2,684**, shortest ch 186
**1,747**. Nothing above the 2,700 guide, nothing near the 1,300 floor; **ch 186 sits below the 1,800
guide**, which `state/book-metadata.md` and this batch's SUMMARY had both claimed was untrue of the whole
batch — corrected in both.

### Held for Volume 5 or later

**What the Foundation stages do to a body that is still being built** (the index finger is the named one
and it is permanent). **What a line of work is worth to the people who live on it.** **Ning Wu and the
register.** **Whether the year of non-forgiveness resolves and what replaces it.** **The claim.** **The
second relicensing's third leg.** **The four months erased in Chapter 3.** **The Clay Plate's texture,
remembered at the last page of the book.** *The shape of a happy life*, spent until Volume 16, by Ning
Wu. **Yuan's origin, Volume 11** — he never tells her what he was before he died before then, and does
not learn she saw him as a readout before then either.

## Established facts, Volume 4 Batch 0003 (ch 209–222) — THE LINE WALKED, AND THE PEOPLE WHO LIVE ON IT

**Fourteen chapters, 28,568 words, average ~2,041** (post-review-repair; pre-repair 28,068 / ~2,005). Day
**281 to day 376**. Longest ch 218 (2,425), shortest
ch 214 (1,666). **Four bracketed layer lines in fourteen chapters, at ch 211, 214, 217 and 220, and zero in
the other ten.** Batch shape is not monotonic (2,038 · 1,872 · 2,078 · **1,809** · **2,247** · **1,666** ·
2,160 · 1,917 · **1,984** · **2,425** · **1,856** · 2,184 · 2,105 · 2,227): the peaks are the noise in the ground
(213) and the ground closing (218); the valleys are the kind word that is not said twice (212), the third
stage (214) and the girl who is idle (219).

### Review repair on this batch — `logs/volume-04-batch-0003.review.log`

**No fact, day, stage, cost, decision or ending changed. Four errors and three craft findings repaired in
place; the batch was not restarted.**

- **ch 209** — a crew of eleven men and a girl can no longer send forty-five men to cells. **"about forty of
  them… and five went to one"** → **"about nine of them… and two went to one."** The course crew is
  **eleven men and a girl of nineteen**, as ch 209's own sixth-hour reading and every later chapter already
  said.
- **ch 217** — the rebuild count. **"four times in her life — once at eleven, once at fifteen, once at
  nineteen, and this was the one that finished"** put one of the four in her future at an age she never
  reaches. **She had done it twice in her life, and this was the one that finished.** This is the only
  standing fact the review changed: **the heel went once, three years and a bit ago, and she put two years
  into rebuilding the order, so the rebuild has happened twice — once, and once more.**
- **ch 219** — **"the hand is the thing that went in June"** → **"…that went on the twenty-fifth."** **No
  calendar month is named anywhere in ch 209–222**, and the only time-keeping in the batch is the ordinal day
  of the month. The twenty-fifth is the day ch 217 gives.
- **ch 221** — a garbled clause, *"in the two weeks that followedowing two weeks"*, repaired to *"in the two
  weeks that followed."*
- **Speech.** **All fourteen chapters now carry quoted speech.** Five (212, 214, 217, 219, 220) carried none
  in the first draft. The added exchanges are ordinary camp speech and **change nothing**: the trestle talk
  about the thirty-nine while she waits (212) · the girl of nineteen naming that she is stopping on every
  pull (214) · a man asking why she stops and being told her hand is slow (217) · a man asking the foreman
  whether the stretch comes back, and the girl asking whether the forty will be put on (219) · the
  foreman's daughter asking him in the open what the line through the name is (220).
- **Restatement cut** in ch 213 and ch 212, and **twenty-six argument-label headings removed**, 116 down to
  90, 6.4 a chapter.

### The clock, as drafted

- **Day 0 is the last day of the thaw. Ch 208 ended on day 280. Ch 209 is day 281 and ch 222 is day 376.**
  Nothing falls between batches. **Foundation 2 at day 296 · 3 at day 316 · 4 at day 338 · 5 at day 360** —
  intervals of twenty, twenty, twenty-two and twenty-two days from the one before, as `outline/volume-04.md`
  gives them.
- **Month-day = `((day − 134) mod 30) + 1` on a thirty-day month.** Day 281 is the 28th and day 283 is the 30th; **day 284 is
  the first** and day 314 and day 344 are the firsts after that. **The three attendances in this batch are
  day 299, day 329 and day 359 — all the sixteenth, thirty days apart.** Day 299 is the scene at ch 212; the
  day 329 sheet and the day 359 sheet each go west in **one sentence** inside ch 215 and ch 219.
- **The carrier came back east about day 286, the third of the month**, with the county's round. The run
  after that one is a man who runs his own book and leaves when he leaves, **and after him there is no date
  west on that board for the rest of the season.** Spent at ch 210 and not used again.
- **No weekday is named anywhere in ch 209–222.** ***Eleven weeks*: zero** — it appeared three times in draft
  and every one was cut. **The four seconds: zero** — it appeared twice in draft and both were cut.

### The money, as drafted

- **The tin is gone since the evening of day 268** and is not named, counted or revived anywhere in this
  batch. About **ten coppers in hand** at the end of it.
- **Six coppers a day and a place at the fire at the course**, and the fire and the bunk and the bread all
  come out of the same six — **one number, not two** (ch 211).
- **Foundation 2 was bought with five days of the six coppers — thirty coppers — and it was paid for by not
  putting a share into the cook-fire.** She stopped putting in on the eighth of the month, paid at the plank
  on the twelfth, and the fire was not lit out of her share from the thirteenth. **About eleven cold days in
  two stretches.** She then settled permanently on **her own bread bought at the store and no share in a
  fire at all**, and that arrangement holds to the end of the batch.
- **From ch 221 the rate is five coppers and not six**, because a sub-contractor pays what a sub-contractor
  pays. **Six is the rate at the course and five is the rate off it, the difference is never explained, and
  it is not a mystery.**

### What the batch established on the page

1. **A line of work is kept by a board and a bar, and the board is the whole of the coordination** (ch 209).
   Readings go up before light; the foreman reads the board with a thumb and sends eleven men to cells that
   are empty; a man attends a stretch on the day it is due and not the day it is convenient, and puts fresh
   marks on it as he goes, and **the marking is what tomorrow is made of**. **Nobody upstream of that board
   exists.**
2. **She is the slowest person on the only object there is** (ch 209). A day's line takes her eleven minutes
   where the tender takes four, so the readings go up late on about two days in three. **Nobody is rude,
   nobody is cruel, nobody offers to take a line off her, and nothing about it is ever mentioned again**,
   because a place where people are paid by the day does not stop the line for a slow hand. **The whole
   review is that the tender read a line she wrote and did not change it.**
3. **The road west closed for the season** (ch 210). **The district's answer space took its first entry: a
   date and one office word, *Held*** — the paper is in a room and nothing has been done to it. **She heard
   a second meaning in it, is the only person within nine days of road who could, and told nobody and did not
   put it into a sentence with a verb even in her own head.** **The request and the referral are still
   pending and nothing was answered.**
4. **Foundation 2, day 296**, bought out of wages. **The cost is what the six stops covering.** The bracket
   announces and goes about two hundred yards into a field. **She is not hungry and has not been since the
   first Foundation; she notices it on the day of the stage and does not have time for the thing underneath
   it.**
5. **MEMBER ABSENT for the second time, on the sixteenth, in a column of its own**, with eleven days of good
   hours beside it and a foot and a half of blank paper in the reason column (ch 212). **Ma Sheng did not
   offer the word again.** He read her line, stood there with his hands at his sides, and went and wrote a
   man's hours against a stretch. **Three men had been arguing at that trestle for the half hour before he
   came about whether the thirty-nine reads on the nineteenth, and she stood inside that talk waiting for a
   fourth sentence that did not come.** **The offer at ch 205 was never a rule and never a standing arrangement —
   it was a man, not asked, saying a thing he was entitled to say once.** She learns that she had been
   relying on it, and that it took nine seconds to come down, and that it was built out of nothing.
6. **Thick drift under a bar makes a noise and keeps making it after the bar stops**, low, under the arm,
   for about as long as it takes to count eleven (ch 213). **Eleven men and a girl of nineteen have an
   ordinary working word for it — a stretch that has got loud** — and they say it as a fact about ground.
   Over sixteen days she kept the board against what nine counties have heard, in order, over four years:
   **the loud stretches are nearest the inside end, the worst of them is the worst-worked, and the quietest
   ground on nine days is at the far end, where there is nothing to announce because there is nobody there.**
   **She said it to herself once, in one plain sentence, built no theory on it, told nobody, and did not tell
   him** — he cannot read a board and the only thing he could have done with it was spend something she had
   not got out of her own column. **It cannot be proved: the only control in the world is herself.** Nothing
   in this batch gives the tender's tenth part or proves the crews wrong.
7. **Foundation 3, day 316.** Short breath goes to **four counts on the flat, on the line and on a grade, and
   three is gone and is not coming back.** She says it once, plainly, and does not say it again in this
   batch. **She is stronger on the flat than she has ever been and worse on the ground she is paid for, and
   both of those are true at the same time and there is nobody in nine days of road to tell her which she
   is.** **The girl of nineteen stopped at the end of her eight yards and told her she was stopping on every
   pull — the first true thing anybody has said to her about her body in four years — and she gave her no
   answer, because there was none she could give.**
8. **The Hunter attends for thirteen days, buys nothing, asks nothing, threatens nothing, touches nothing,
   enters nothing, and says in the same breath that he has not asked her a question and is not going to
   start** (ch 215). **A field man of a Warden's office cannot unmake a System inside a bound; that is what a
   Wall writ is for, and a Wall writ is issued by the line's own people to somebody who lives on it, and she
   is not one of them and the line does not know she exists.** What the line can do is **close its own
   working ground against a person it does not carry.** **He does not want it executed. He wants the answer
   pending, and he says so out loud, in the ordinary voice, in front of her.** She saw him write the rule
   down at the wire at about forty feet and understood in the time it takes to put a board down that it was
   over, and did not go and stand in front of him.
9. **The far column of the board** (ch 216): a narrower column past the last stretch the crew works, no
   heading she can read, **nothing on this course fills it**, filled by a strip off a cart from the far end
   of the reach, nine years, nobody able to read it, nobody having ever asked. **She can read the frame and
   not one word of the content, and one line in it is dated the thirteenth of this month — the day she took
   the second Foundation — and is in a form none of the nine years around it is in.** She tells nobody that
   she has told the difference and does not say out loud what the line is. **Her hand was on the Ledger that
   evening and she did not order it** — one sentence of narration, no dialogue, no price, nobody told — and
   **the ease of that is the cold of the chapter.**
10. **Foundation 4, day 338.** **Not the heel — the order.** Two years of rebuilding a hand with no heel
    into a working hand: the wrist already turned, the fingers already closing, the turn before anybody
    thinks. After this she can still hold, carry, pull and work, and **every grip has to be built from
    nothing, one at a time, before the bar lands, and the turn in a ring cannot be built because it has to
    be at the top of the pull.** The distance the order has to travel is four inches. **The writing hand is
    unchanged — the flat of a second finger, eleven minutes** — and she wrote that on the ticket on purpose.
    **The girl of nineteen has the order and is nineteen and is not stupid and is not wrong about any of it.**
    **At the ninth hour a man she had never spoken to put his bar down flat and asked her why she was
    stopping on every pull; she said her hand was slow, and he said his was slow too, and went back into it,
    and asked her nothing else.**
11. **The ground closed (ch 218).** The decision was made at a table in a room at the west end, by the
    foreman, the tender and the man in the plain coat, **and the reason was the Hunter's own referral read
    out of his notebook by him, because a foreman asked a man with a notebook what the paper was and he
    answered him truthfully.** Both halves of the referral are true and both are on the same paper. It
    reached her at a trestle at about the sixth hour **in about nine words in the ordinary voice and
    everybody carried on.** **She said nothing. There is no version of anything she could have said, and
    she had four years of paper behind her and the paper was about to be read correctly.** **Nobody stopped
    her and nothing was taken out of her hands: she is not the kind of person the ground closes on, and
    that is the whole reason it worked.**
12. **What it cost them, shown on the ground and not in a speech** (ch 218–219). **The stretch went back in
    three days** — faster than last time, and she knew the four places where it takes a boot. **The run is
    shorter, the contract is smaller, the board has a hole in it where a mark used to get a figure, nine
    men are idle, and the girl of nineteen is idle because a rule does not make an exception for a foreman's
    daughter either, and she is somebody's daughter.** **The men are slower in that ring than she was and do
    not know it and never will.** **One man asked the foreman outright, on the fourteenth, whether the
    stretch would come back on, and got no reason and no tone. The girl of nineteen asked Ning Yan, on the
    other side of a cart wheel on the ninth, whether the forty would be put on before the month turns, and
    was told no, and said she would ask her father, and then that he would say he did not know.**
13. **She stood outside the wire alone in the worst ground on nine days for about an hour and a half** (ch
    219). **The boot went. She put her weight over the pull. She did not fall, nothing broke, nobody was
    hurt, nobody came.** She got back over the wire undramatically and nobody saw it. **She was jealous of
    nine men who are going to be measurably worse at a job she is not allowed to do, and being ashamed of it
    did not stop it.**
14. **Her name was struck out of a contractor's wage-book** (ch 220), in a foreman's own hand, in the open,
    at the second hour of the seventeenth, with a stroke of a pen, and **he did not do it at night and he
    did not do it in a room**. **The hours to that day stand; the rate stops; the book does not close.** It
    is **the first time in four years a piece of paper has been about her that was written by a person who
    does not know her.** **The Hunter was there, wrote it down afterward, and said nothing to her.** **His
    daughter asked him, ten feet off and in the open, what the line was; he told her once and flat that the
    hours are in the book and stay in it and that a hole in the book is a hole in it, and she said *Oh* and
    went and got her bar, and nobody looked at Ning Yan while any of it happened.**
15. **Foundation 5, day 360 — the same week, and the two things are not connected** (ch 220). She had two
    days to be certain of that and being certain was worse than not knowing, because if the stage had cost
    her the name she could have carried both as one thing. **The relief is real and is not defeat: as long as
    her name was in that book it was the only book in the world with her name in it, and now there is a
    second possibility.** She is eighteen, standing at a trestle, and she is the most expensive worker on a
    ground that is shut.
16. **The batch's irreversible decision (ch 221).** **She hires on under a sub-contractor at five coppers.**
    His book has four columns and a painted mark, he is not answerable to the foreman, **he does not know why
    the course closed and does not ask, because a man who runs work out past the last stake has never asked a
    hired hand why she came.** **From that hour two books in two hands hold her name and neither can be
    checked against the other, and the hours split so neither carries a continuous run — the exact instrument
    the whole relicensing was built out of.** She took it apart with her own mouth at a trestle in daylight
    and nobody had to be persuaded. **It is the best deal she has been offered in four volumes and she knows
    exactly what it is.**
17. **A stretch nobody is hired on** was named by the sub-contractor in the ordinary way — the worst ground on
    nine days, on nobody's book, nobody's job — **and she did not go on it and did not ask about it** (ch 221).
    It is **Batch 0004's** and it is named in this batch and nothing else.
18. **The far side is somebody's ordinary country and nobody is waiting for her** (ch 222). Five coppers for a
    cart. Drift thin, no ring, a bar into six inches that does not ring. **Wei Ran, twenty, mortal-born, one
    team out past the far end of the working reach and back** — the first person Ning Yan has met who went
    through without permission. **Asked one direct question, she said so in four words and did not
    elaborate.** She said the correct thing about the line **as a fact and not as comfort**, and it was right,
    and it is a thing Ning Yan did not know. **She goes off page at the end of the chapter.**
19. **There is no word within nine days of road for either of the two things she has learned this season**
    (ch 222): **the noise, and the quiet.** Nine counties has a word for *servant-born*, for *irregular*, for
    *the Chosen One*, and for the eleventh of a list of eleven designations, **and there is nothing anywhere
    for a woman whose advancement has leaked into the ground of people who never heard it.**

### Construction budget — spent in Batch 0003, not to reuse

Bracketed layer lines: **four in fourteen chapters, at ch 211, 214, 217 and 220**, and **zero in the other
ten**. Each names her, names her county, names Foundation, names the stage and stops — **no location, no
advice, no explanation, and nothing about what heard it.** The seven relicensing conditions re-listed:
**zero** — the licence and the claim are referenced and never told. The suppression arithmetic: **zero.** The
signed-shift roster rule: **zero.** ***There is no form*: zero. *There is no column*: zero** — and ch 216
has a column that exists and has been full for nine years, which is the opposite of the construction.
***The record remained*: zero.** ***Nobody asked*: zero** in plain form. ***Nobody was thanked*: zero** —
the construction is spent at ch 200 on the three sponsors and this batch reaches the same territory through
*the offer that was not made a second time* and *a word read out of a board for nothing*, which are different
constructions and both of them are on the page once. *Nobody knew*: **zero**. *Nobody said anything*: **zero**
in plain form. ***Nobody else alive* used three times in one paragraph in draft at ch 213 and all three were
cut.** *The line*, *tending*, *the course* and *drift* are spent words from ch 199 and are **used and never
re-explained**; the chapter that describes the line doing its work (ch 209) says nothing about either side of
anything and **nothing in this batch proves the crews wrong or supplies the tenth part.** *A Wall writ* is
named once as an instrument, in his mouth, at ch 215, and **the Wall itself is not described.** **No office
named. No new final enemy. Nothing Volume 5 owns is spent.** *The shape of a happy life*: zero.
***Kiln-born* and the eleven designations: **not on the page** (the eleven designations are referred to as a
list of eleven and are not listed). The **right index finger**: **named nowhere, priced nowhere,
foreshadowed nowhere.** Weekdays: **zero named.** ***Eleven weeks*: zero. The four seconds: zero.** Banned
reflexes: **zero.** Authorial scaffolding: **zero** — *this chapter*, *this volume*, *this batch* and *on the
page* each leaked into draft in four chapters and every one was cut. Nothing is restated in meta terms: the
seven relicensing conditions, the nine/four county rule (stated once at ch 211 in the form the close review
set), the reason-column rule (referenced at ch 212 in new words and not re-taught), the counterfoil
(referenced at ch 208's block and not re-described), the ch 150 refusal (not touched, not softened, not
regretted, and no line lets her wonder what she would have done). Present perfect and simple present on
Ning Yan and on named characters in narration was swept across all fourteen and reduced to the structural
present about forms, offices, counties and classes of person, which is house voice and stays.

### Carried forward, unchanged, into Batch 0004 (ch 223–238)

- **FOUNDATION 5.** Ceiling on the front of the relicensed licence **still reads Foundation the first stage**
  and she has still never touched it. **Foundation 6 at ch 224 (day 386) · 7 at ch 228 (day 412) · 8 at
  ch 234 (day 440, SUPPRESSED at her own order, no bracketed line in the chapter) · 9 at ch 237 (day 462,
  the announcement that goes past the line).** **Ch 236 is the first page on which Yuan reads the office name
  above this county.**
- **Short breath four counts everywhere, three is gone. The order is gone out of the left hand. The heel is
  gone and that is not what either stage cost. The writing hand is unchanged — eleven minutes with the flat of
  a second finger.** The **right index finger** is unspent and unnamed.
- **MEMBER ABSENT**, on her member's sheet, separately from her hours, not payable in hours, permanent, with
  a blank reason column going west for the second time. **Nothing in Batch 0004 may undo it.**
- **A relicensing complete in three legs, all outside this county**, in the order clearance → seal →
  countersignature. **Not re-told, not re-listed.** The claim stands with its date. Hou Sanliang is unpaid,
  patient and never met.
- **The course is closed against her lawfully and correctly, off page, at her own cost and at theirs.** The
  ground is shut; the board has a hole in it; nine men and a girl of nineteen are idle; the stretch she kept
  went back in three days.
- **Two books in two hands now hold her name**, one at six coppers on the course's ground and one at five
  past the last stake, and **neither carries a continuous run of her hours.** This is the damage the
  irreversible decision did and it is Batch 0004's standing condition.
- **Four counterfoils** — the only object in the world that can certify an hour she worked out there — plus
  dead weight tickets with a ration's marks on them, a note of four stage tests and a note of the cost of the
  fifth stage.
- **Cui Bao**, **Ma Sheng**, **the girl of nineteen** and eleven men, who are right, and whom nothing in
  this volume proves wrong. **Their own win happened and it cost them.**
- **The Hunter's win landed and he does not want it.** He wants the answer pending. **He is not named in
  this batch and no document he signs carries a name.**
- **What she knows about the drift and has told nobody** (ch 213): the loud stretches are nearest the inside
  end, the quietest ground on nine days is at the far end where there is nobody to be announced. **Unprovable
  and unprovable-looking.** Batch 0004 owns what she does with it.
- **The last column on the board**, nine years of it, one line dated the day she took the second Foundation,
  in a hand from past the last stake, **and she does not know what the line is.** **Batch 0004's and Volume
  6–10's.**
- **The empty answer space is no longer empty**: a date and *Held*, and **that is not an answer to the
  request.** The referral and the request are **still pending**. **Batch 0004 puts the countersignature on her
  petition into the space and that is not an answer either, and the volume's last joke.**
- **Wei Ran**, twenty, mortal-born, a carter with one team, **off page at ch 222.**
- **The stretch nobody is hired on**, named at ch 221 and not entered. **The blind run is Batch 0004's.**
- **About ten coppers, bread bought at a store, no share in a fire, five coppers a day.**
- **The channel: four ration spends in fourteen chapters, two of which bought nothing, and no figure of what
  is left stated anywhere in ch 209–222.** Ledger not ordered; the standing used once, at ch 216, without
  explanation. **Merit: none. Nothing in this batch kills anything or clears a listed trial, and the channel
  does not come back.** Batch 0004 clears the trial and restores it at a price out of her foundation.
- **The register still reads *reconciled*. The Clay Plate still worn. Shen Xiu unforgiven and unthanked. The
  three sponsors silent and their second book lodged nowhere and spoken of nowhere. What became of the eleven
  is not raised. Ning Wu does not appear. Nine days of road behind her and nothing on it has told her
  anything.** **She does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop.**

### Volume 4 running ledger — at end of ch 222

| Item | Value at end of ch 222 |
| --- | --- |
| Her stage | **FOUNDATION 5, reached day 360 and announced at her own order.** The paper still says Foundation the first stage. Short breath four counts on the flat, on the line and on a grade; three is gone. The order is gone out of the left hand; the hand still holds, carries and pulls, and every grip is built from nothing. The heel of the left hand is gone and is not the cost; every county hour is still eleven minutes with the flat of a second finger. Right arm open a third of the time. Aperture cracked through and permanent. Not hungry since the first Foundation. Not in the cook-fire; her own bread at the store |
| Licence | **Relicensed on the second set of legs, all three outside this county** (clearance 264, seal 265, countersignature 266). **A document she has never touched.** Seven conditions referenced and never re-listed; the claim the seventh and last. The first licence is still rolled in the lining of her coat; the second is still nailed straight above the second bunk, nine days west. The Foundation petition is barred, not refused, and not withdrawable |

| Hours | **Six coppers a day on the course's ground until the seventeenth of a month, and five coppers a day at a sub-contractor's trestle from the twenty-first.** Two books, two hands, the hours split, **and neither carries a continuous run.** Her name is out of the course's book. **Four counterfoils in the oilcloth** |

Merit: none. **He does not grow back and he is quieter at the end of this batch than at the start** |
| Bounty | 800 jin, standing, unanswered, the seventh and last condition, travelling on the licence. The date is still in the counting room in Lowwater. The hand is still named and has been found and answered nothing. Hou Sanliang unpaid, patient, never met |
| The referral | Up the stair past every office she has ever stood in front of. **Its answer space took its first entry: a date and the office word *Held* — the paper is held and nothing has been done to it.** The request is still pending and is not answered anywhere in this batch |
| Crew | **nine men and her; ten sleepers in twelve bunks, nine days west.** Not in this batch and not mentioned. Gao kept the second bunk. Qin walked her to the dressed stone. **She has sent nothing and received nothing** |

Nine men idle, the girl idle, the contract smaller, the board with a hole in it, the stretch she kept back in three days. **They are right** |
| Past the last stake | **A sub-contractor with a book of his own, four columns, a painted mark, four men and sometimes six, five coppers and a place at the fire.** A carter will take a person out past the last stake for money. **Wei Ran, twenty, mortal-born, one team out past the far end of the reach and back, off page.** A stretch nobody is hired on, named and not entered |
| Register / plate | *reconciled* / worn. **She does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop.** The year was not touched in this batch |
| Decision taken | **She has split her hours across two books in two hands so that neither of them can carry a continuous run of her work, and she did it on purpose, in daylight, at a trestle, and nobody had to be persuaded.** She is outside the wire, five coppers a day, eighteen years old, and she is holding a fact about the drift that eleven men could not work out and that she can never prove and has told nobody |
