## Established facts, Volume 4 complete (ch 179–238) — THE COLD-READER STANDING RECORD

**Volume 4 is written to its last chapter.** This block is the whole volume in one place and it is what the
Volume 4 close audit walks, and what Volume 5's planner reads first. The per-batch blocks below it stay as
the detailed record; nothing here replaces them.

**Sixty chapters, 120,566 words, day 220 to day 470.** Batch 0001 ch 179–192 (28,608 words) · 0002 ch
193–208 (31,941) · 0003 ch 209–222 (28,568) · 0004 ch 223–238 (31,490, written in two halves). **Five
bracketed layer lines in Batches 0001–0003 (207, 211, 214, 217, 220), three in Batch 0004 (224, 228
and 237), and eight in the volume:** Foundation 1 (207), 2 (211), 3 (214), 4 (217), 5 (220), 6 (224), 7
(228), and 9 (237). Foundation 8 (234) was suppressed at her own order and its chapter carries no bracket
at all.**

### What the volume did, in one paragraph

**She was out of the execution range of a dismantling order because she was inside the bound, and the only
countersignature that could take her ceiling up was past a boundary no county paper crosses, and she could
not have the counter and not the execution.** She went nine days east to a line of work, was hired by the day
at five coppers, relicensed at Foundation the first stage on a schedule read aloud out of a contractor's
wage-book, took four stages in a season at four separate prices, split her own hours across two books on
purpose and lost the run she had paid for, hired onto the stretch nobody is hired on, cleared it in fourteen
days by hand, **paid a channel back out of a base one stage short of finished and lost the right index
finger for it**, suppressed a stage into a gap on a board the far side keeps, took the ninth out loud where
nothing is announced, **was heard a mile past the post by a country that has never heard one, and crossed** —
first as a dated line the layer put in a book she was not standing in, then as a body, three-quarters of a
mile of quiet ground and a day and a half.

### The stages, all nine

| Stage | Ch | Day | Bracket | What it cost, named once |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Foundation 1 | 207 | 276 | yes | the relicensing's hours read out of a wage-book this county does not hold; four weeks of a body never full and cold at once |
| Foundation 2 | 211 | 296 | yes | she bought the materials out of a tin she had to rebuild twice |
| Foundation 3 | 214 | 316 | yes | short breath to four counts, and it never came back |
| Foundation 4 | 217 | 338 | yes | the left-hand order, two years of rebuilding, gone and not coming back |
| Foundation 5 | 220 | 360 | yes | a name struck out of a wage-board in a foreman's own hand |
| Foundation 6 | 224 | 386 | yes | **the sound in their ears becomes a line on a board and the people on the line can tell a stage from a bill** |
| Foundation 7 | 228 | 412 | yes | **three days no book can find, and eleven men who stand a stretch for nothing** |
| **Foundation 8** | **234** | **440** | **NONE — suppressed at her own order** | **what the filing records is an hour worked, and the board on the far side records an absence of the same width** |
| **Foundation 9** | **237** | **462** | **yes — and it went past the line** | **the announcement goes past the line**, and the far side writes it in its own board |

**Intervals of twenty, twenty, twenty-two, twenty-two, twenty-six, twenty-six, twenty-eight and twenty-two
days — lengthening through the middle, shorter at the last because the last one was not carried alone.** No
skipped breakthrough, none prepared early, **no tribulation anywhere in the volume.**

### The five irreversible decisions of Batch 0004

1. **Ch 233 — she hired onto the stretch nobody is hired on and held the standing for the crossing.** The
   blind run is on nobody's book, paid worst, and the layer lists it because the layer lists the jobs that
   settle a debt. **Her name said out loud for the third time on that ground, at a trestle, in daylight,
   nobody persuaded.** The price had been said before the word at ch 231: **one day out of the three she
   was keeping, held under a day, put down when the crossing was over.**
2. **Ch 234 — she suppressed Foundation 8 at her own order.** No bracketed line in the chapter at all. **The
   hole is on a board she cannot read and it is there for nine years.**
3. **Ch 235 — she cleared the trial knowing what the spending came out of.** **Merit settled on a finished
   job and not on a dead man; he intercepted a share and spent it out of her foundation while the base was
   one stage short of finished, and it did not come back. The right index finger went, permanently, at the
   blind run.** She told the crew out loud, in the ordinary voice, because the ch 172 precedent is a
   precedent now.
4. **Ch 237 — she announced Foundation 9 at her own order where nothing is announced.** The sound went past
   the line because the drift carries it, and the far side wrote it down because that is what the board is
   for. **It cannot be unheard past the line.**
5. **Ch 238 — she wrote her name in a book on the far side.** With the flat of a second finger and four
   fingers' worth of grip left on the other hand, saying the price out loud because that is what is said on
   a line. **From that hour she is across, on a wage-board the far side keeps, in a column that is hers.**

### Yuan's economy across the volume

**Ledger taken twice, refused once out loud, and hers to order at the last page.** Batch 0001: **eight days
out of ten**, for the hand that wrote a date in Hou Sanliang's counting room — Luo Ping, thirty-nine, with a
wife and a boy of eleven past this county's boundary. **Refused once, out loud, at a moment it would have
been easy**, and used once without a price. Batch 0004: **one day out of three, stated before the word at
ch 231, the word said at ch 233, held under a day, put down when the crossing was over.**

**The channel: about ten days at ch 178; eight spent in Batch 0001; about two days rationed to roughly four
sentences a day that she chose and counted, with no figure of what was left stated anywhere until ch 236;
and about six weeks at the last page, restored out of her foundation at ch 235.** **Merit settled once in
the whole volume, on a cleared listed trial, and nothing in Volume 4 killed anybody.**

**What he can read changed once.** He had said four times that he could not read the office name above this
county. **At ch 236 he read it out loud in one breath: the Standing Office of the Second Account.** A middle
office, with others above it. **He stopped there.** No Scribe, no Anchor, nothing about what he was before he
died, and no page before ch 236 names it.

### Fifteen required continuity facts, at the last page

1. **Foundation 9, licensed by the second relicensing, countersigned past the county's boundary in an
   office's hand and not a person's, on a schedule this county does not hold, read out of a contractor's
   wage-book.** The paper still says Foundation the first stage and **she has still never touched it.**
2. **The right index finger — gone, permanently, at the far end of the blind run, at about the sixth hour on
   the twenty-first of a month, told out loud to the crew in the ordinary voice.** The finger that makes the
   turn at the end of a stroke, and the finger a licence nail goes in with. **Four fingers' worth of grip left
   on that hand. Nothing in the book gives it back and nothing offers to.**
3. **The relicensing legs, in order: clearance (264) → seal (265) → countersignature (266).** Not inverted.
4. **What the boundary is, in the terms Volume 4 earned:** a line of work, tended by hand, paid by the day;
   drift; the residue of everything the bound is told, running along it and pooling where the ground is soft
   and thickening where the work was worst. **Ninety parts true. The tenth part is not given.** And the layer
   logs a thing leaving a place, which is why a board nine years old on the far side has a dated column with
   her hand in it and nobody else's.
5. **What crossing cost, in hours and in people:** eleven men and a girl stood a stretch for three days for
   nothing and told her nothing; **three empty dates that are true in a wage-book at a trestle**; nine men
   idle and a girl of nineteen idle and she is somebody's daughter; a name struck out of a contractor's
   wage-book; and the crew who were told out loud about a finger.
6. **The claim: STANDING AND MOVED, NOT ANSWERED.** Eight hundred jin, the seventh and last condition,
   travelling on a licence nobody has touched. **At ch 237 somebody on the far side read the seventh condition
   out loud in a room**, which put it into the first office within nine days of road that can act on it in
   its whole life. **Hou Sanliang unpaid, patient and never met. A date is still entered against it in a
   counting room in Lowwater and has not been torn off.**
7. **The referral and the empty space:** the space took a **date and the office word *Held*** at ch 210, and
   **at ch 238 the countersignature on her petition**, in an office's hand, dated from the far side. **It is
   not an answer to the request. The request is still pending — a Wall writ is not issued to a field agent,
   and the line's own people still do not know she exists.**
8. **Feng's Lung: the first is spent (ch 143), the second is Volume 11 and is unspent.** What Volume 4 took
   from him instead: his fair hand, his stair, his quarter. **Alive, warm, column at five, not thanked.**
9. ***Kiln-born* is still in two heads and on no paper. The office above this county is NO LONGER UNREADABLE
   — read at ch 236, a middle office with others above it, and everything above that one is still unread.**
10. **The register still reads *reconciled*.**
11. **The plate is still worn**, against her ribs, above the oilcloth, under six counterfoils and three dead
    weight tickets.
12. **Ledger: held and refused, and held and spent. Taken twice in Volume 4 and refused once out loud.** Eight
    days out of ten for a name; **one day out of three for the crossing**; refused once at a moment it would
    have been easy. **Hers to order at the last page and she does not order it.**
13. **The year of non-forgiveness: CHANGED SHAPE AND STILL RUNNING.** It is not forgiveness and the account
    is not closed. It is a purchase, and `outline/ending.md` is fixed that she forgives him in ch 1000 and it
    is housekeeping that closes because he is gone.
14. **The channel: about six weeks at the last page**, restored by a listed trial cleared and not by a
    killing, out of a base that was one stage short of finished when he spent it.
15. **Wei Ran: appeared once in Batch 0004, at ch 229, and is off page.** Twenty coppers and a road, not a
    favour; the true thing about the bad flat; **nothing is a romance.**

### Hard things the volume did not do

- It did not answer the Hunter's request in any form, and did not soften, defer, reopen or regret the ch 150
  refusal.
- It did not retire the non-forgiveness, and did not undo MEMBER ABSENT.
- It did not answer what became of the eleven, and did not break the silence bought at ch 128.
- It did not resolve the claim, tear off the date, put Hou Sanliang in a room, or re-list the seven
  relicensing conditions except once, where the far side reads the seventh out loud.
- It did not name the Scribe or the Anchor, did not tell her what he was before he died, and did not let her
  tell him she had seen him as a readout.
- It did not kill anybody, did not turn the county, the registrar, Shen Xiu, the sponsors, Feng, the sub-
  contractor or the crews into villains, and **did not prove the crews wrong.**
- It introduced no new final enemy and spent nothing Volume 5 owns.

### Construction budget, spent in ch 179–238 and not available in plain form again

**Bracketed layer lines: eight in sixty, one at each announced stage, and none at all in ch 234.** The seven
relicensing conditions: **never re-listed**, except the seventh read aloud once on the far side at ch 237. The
four conditions of the Volume 2 licence: never re-listed. The suppression arithmetic: spent three times in
Volume 3 and **said once, in new words, at ch 234, and not argued again.** What a surety is for: spent.
***About four seconds*: eight across ch 179–238 — three in Batch 0001 (ch 181 twice, ch 191), four in Batch
0002 (ch 198, ch 205, ch 208 twice), one at ch 212 in Batch 0003, none in Batch 0004 and none in the last
sixteen chapters. A declining rhythm, not a metronome, and not a number to reach for again.*** ***Eleven
weeks*: zero.**** ***Nobody asked, nobody was thanked, there is no
form, there is no column, the record remained, nobody knew, nobody said anything*: all spent or revoiced
above them, and none available plain.** *The shape of a happy life*: zero. Authorial scaffolding: zero.
Banned reflexes: zero.

### VOLUME 4 CLOSE AUDIT — CORRECTIONS TO THIS FILE (ch 179–238), written at the volume close

**`outline/volume-04.md` walked line by line against the manuscript. Four faults were repaired in the
chapters; five findings are recorded with a reason; three inaccurate claims in the state files are corrected
here and in `state/current.md`, `state/open-threads.md` and `state/book-metadata.md`. The full table is
`## Volume 4 close audit — CLOSED` in `state/current.md`. Nothing below replaces any per-batch block.**

**Corrections to this file.**

1. **THE FOUR SECONDS.** The line above used to read *"The four seconds: never used as a metronome anywhere
   in the volume."* **That was not accurate.** *About four seconds* appears **eight times** in ch 179–238:
   **ch 181 (×2), 191, 198, 205, 208 (×2), 212.** **Batch attribution, as measured against the manuscript
   by grep of `chapters/volume-04/`: three are in Batch 0001 (ch 181 ×2, ch 191), four in Batch 0002
   (ch 198, ch 205, ch 208 ×2), one in Batch 0003 (ch 212), and there are none in Batch 0004.** The
   sentence as first written said nine and put ch 198 in twice, once as a section heading, and the first
   repair kept the nine while re-partitioning the uses; **the count is eight and ch 198 is one prose line.**
   It is a declining rhythm and not a metronome, and no other pause figure is displaced by it — the
   volume's own alternatives are *as long as it takes to count eleven*, *about nine seconds*, *about two
   seconds*, *a second and a half* and *four minutes*. **The rule stands and is unchanged: do not let it
   become a metronome.** The record is corrected to say what is on the page, and `outline/volume-05.md`,
   `outline/batches/volume-05-batch-0001.md` and `state/current.md` have been brought into line with it.
   **The budget line immediately above this block was still reading *nine* when the Volume 5 Batch 0001
   planning review reached it, and has now been corrected in place to eight and re-partitioned 3 / 4 / 1 / 0
   — so this correction is no longer outstanding anywhere and the sentence a drafter reads at the foot of
   the volume now agrees with this one.**
2. **THE WEEKDAY.** *No calendar month and no weekday is named anywhere in ch 179–238* **is true.** Ch 224
   carried the volume's only instance — *I would do the whole of it again on Friday* — and **the close audit
   repaired it in place to *on any working morning*.** The exception no longer stands. **The close review then
   found that the rule had been inferred from this one volume, written nowhere in `bible/` or `outline/`, and
   enforced at that single line while eight standing instances survived in Volumes 1 and 2. The rule is now
   canon in `bible/world.md` (*Rules of the world that hold everywhere* and *What must never appear*) and all
   eight were repaired — see the close-review block beneath this one.**
3. **THE MONTH RULE.** The rule as it stood read *"Days 374 to 463 read day − 373; day 464 begins the next
   month and reads day − 463."* **That is wrong for days 404 onward.** The correct statement is
   **`((day − 134) mod 30) + 1`,** which is the formula already given above it and which agrees with every
   month-day on the page: **days 374–403 read day − 373 · 404–433 read day − 403 · 434–463 read day − 433 ·
   464 onward reads day − 463.** Under the wrong form, days 412 and 440 and 462 and 470 came out as 39, 67,
   89 and 97; under the correct form they are the ninth, the seventh, the twenty-ninth and the seventh,
   which is what ch 228, ch 234, ch 237 and ch 238 each say in the chapter.
4. **THE WORD COUNTS.** The volume file set was recorded as 120,566. **It was 120,571 after the close
   repairs, chapter titles and `##` section headings excluded** — which is the method every per-batch figure
   uses, and the reason the raw `wc -w` count is higher. Per batch, headings excluded: **28,607 · 31,900 ·
   28,580 · 31,484.** The per-batch summary figures in `workspace/` differ from these by a few dozen words each
   and are historical records of the moment each batch closed; **the audited figures are the ones above.**
   **CORRECTED AGAIN BY THE CLOSE REVIEW: the ch 220 sentence it repaired is one word longer than the text it
   replaced, so the volume is 120,572 and Batch 0003 is 28,581.**

**Repairs made in the chapters at the volume close.**

- **ch 224** — the only named weekday in the volume (*on Friday*) → **on any working morning**.
- **ch 232** — *she had four fingers' worth of nothing on the other one that she had not noticed yet* → **and
  every part of her that had gone wrong since the thaw had gone wrong in that hand.** A hand-scoped echo of
  the right index finger, three chapters before ch 235. **The first form of this repair — *…on that side* — was
  itself wrong and was corrected by the close review; see the block beneath this one.**
- **ch 179** — *that is the finger that can still do a turn at the end of a stroke* → **because a coin will go
  across the flat of that one and not across a fingertip.** The construction belonged to ch 235 and was being
  spent fifty-six chapters early.
- **ch 220** — **the fifth stage was dated *the nineteenth of this month*, which is day 362 and makes the
  interval twenty-four days.** **The pinned day is 360, which is the seventeenth**, twenty-two days from the
  twenty-fifth of the month before, and **the chapter's own title — *Struck Out* — and nine later chapters all
  date the strike to the seventeenth.** Corrected to **the seventeenth**, with *it came in the same week* →
  **it came earlier in that same day** and the two *two days* recast as **the rest of the day** and **the hours
  between the two of them.** The chapter's argument — that the two things are not connected — is preserved and
  sharpens, because the stage and the strike are now hours apart on one day.

**Findings recorded and not repaired, with the reason.**

1. **Bai Zhu's name is spoken twice more than the outline's *named once*:** in narration at ch 189 (×3) and
   by Ning Yan's own mouth at ch 196. **The operative rule — no document he signs carries his name — is intact
   and confirmed at ch 220 and ch 238**, and both spoken uses were authorised by Volume 4's own batch cards.
2. **The ch 234 cost is echoed at ch 236, not at ch 235.** Each of the four costs is named exactly twice in
   the volume and never a third time.
3. **Feng's fair hand copying the Foundation column, and his copies going past the line into a book the far
   side keeps, are not on the page.** He appears once in the whole volume, at ch 181, and Volume 4's Batch
   0003 and Batch 0004 cards forbade him appearing without a card. **Carried unspent into Volume 5.**
4. **Bai Zhu's win at ch 218 stands and is not escalated**, and he is not a villain at any point.
5. **The far-side keeper's reason for giving her a knife at ch 236 is an outside man's eye catching how she
   holds her right hand, and is recorded here because it is the only sentence in the volume that could be
   read as pricing the finger a second time.**

**The fifteen required continuity facts are confirmed one at a time, and three of them have changed shape.**
The three, stated in the form the last page actually used:

- **Item 9 — the unreadable office. IT IS NO LONGER UNREADABLE.** Yuan read it at ch 236, in one breath:
  **the Standing Office of the Second Account**, a middle office with others above it. **What is still
  unreadable is everything above that one.** *Kiln-born* is still in two heads and on no paper and returned
  zero across ch 119–238.
- **Item 12 — Ledger. Taken twice in Volume 4 and refused once out loud.** Batch 0001: eight days out of ten
  for a name. Batch 0001: refused once, out loud, at a moment it would have been easy. Batch 0004: one day out
  of three for a crossing, held under a day and put down when the crossing was over. **It is hers to order at
  the last page and she does not order it.**
- **Item 13 — the year of non-forgiveness. Changed shape and still running.** It is not forgiveness, it is a
  purchase, and the account is carrying. The sentence the last page actually used is ch 230's: *That is not
  forgiveness. It is a purchase, and the account is still carrying, and it will be carrying at the end of the
  book.* **Do not retire it.** `outline/ending.md` is fixed that she forgives him in ch 1000 and it is
  housekeeping that closes because he is gone.

**The protagonist question, answered at the close.** **Yuan is the book's protagonist and he has been a voice
only, off-panel, for two volumes of sixteen.** What he did across ch 179–238, all of it his own decisions:
he **found a hand that wrote a date against a claim**; he **held a standing for a crossing and was paid out of
a base that was not finished**; he **intercepted a share of a settled job and spent it**; he **restored his own
life**; and he **read one name he had said four times he could not read, in one breath, and stopped.** **Every
one of them was paid for by her, in hours, in a stage, or in a finger. That is not enough, and the finding is
stated without softening: sixty chapters of close third on a woman who is not the protagonist, in which the
protagonist has no scene, no body, no room and nobody to speak to, is a structural debt two volumes of sixteen
old. Volume 5 takes the correction inside its own sixty chapters — at least one sustained scene in which Yuan
acts, or is acted on, away from her hearing — and it takes something that is his, because a man the reader has
never seen in a room cannot carry the fall `outline/ending.md` fixes for him. Full statement in
`state/current.md` under *The protagonist, answered in writing*.**

### VOLUME 4 CLOSE REVIEW REPAIR — what the review found and what was changed

**The close was audited line by line, all word counts and the day arithmetic reproduced, and the markdown, the
titles and the bracketed-line budget all verified clean. Five findings were raised. Four were real and are
repaired here; one is operational and is recorded for the controller. No beat, title, order, ending or planned
plot was touched, and nothing a batch or volume owns was taken.**

1. **ch 220 kept the old timing in one sentence.** The close moved the stage and the strike into one day —
   the stage at about the fourth hour, the strike in the second hour of the afternoon, *the rest of the day was
   enough*, *the hours between the two of them* — and left *They were two things that happened in the **same
   week*** four paragraphs later, which cancels the reason the repair was made. **Repaired to *They were two
   things that happened inside one day, hours apart*.** Nothing else in the chapter carries the old span.
2. **ch 232's first repair claimed more than the body allows.** *Every part of her that had gone wrong since
   the thaw had gone wrong on that side* is contradicted by two established losses that are not lateral: the
   breath down to four counts and the loss of appetite, both from ch 214. **Repaired to *…had gone wrong in
   that hand***, which is what the fact supports — the order out of the left hand, the heel gone with it, and
   a right hand that is whole until ch 235 — and which still scopes the sentence away from the finger.
3. **The weekday rule was unwritten and unenforced outside one volume.** It is now **canon in
   `bible/world.md`**, in *Rules of the world that hold everywhere* and again under *What must never appear*:
   **the world has no named weeks and no named weekdays; narration may not name one.** The eight standing
   instances in Volumes 1 and 2 were repaired at word level — **ch 40** (*I was worse on **Tuesday*** → *I was
   worse three days ago*) · **ch 47** (*repeated in a wash house by **Friday*** → *by the end of the week*) ·
   **ch 104** (*one in you since **Tuesday*** → *one in you for days*) · **ch 109** (*on the shelf since
   **Tuesday*** → *on the shelf since it came down with the crate*) · **ch 111** (*since a **Tuesday** afternoon
   in the spring* → *since an afternoon in the spring*; *since **Tuesday** at the wash house* → *since that
   afternoon at the wash house*) · **ch 114** (*holding since a **Thursday** in the autumn* → *holding since the
   autumn*; *a registrar deciding on a **Tuesday*** → *on a morning in a month*). **A grep of `chapters/` for
   all seven weekday names now returns zero across ch 1–238.** **Reopening closed chapters is not new to this
   book — Volume 3's Batch 0001 review reopened two Volume 2 chapters, ch 116 and ch 118, over a date that
   contradicted the channel's own figure. This is the first reopening made to apply a canon rule across
   volumes**, because the rule had never been written down anywhere.
   **Two closed batch cards still describe beats in weekday terms and neither beat is on the page:**
   `outline/batches/volume-01-batch-0003.md` (*It is a normal Tuesday*) and `outline/batches/volume-04-batch-0002.md`
   (*a matter of a man's Tuesday*). They are left as historical planning records. **The rule in `bible/world.md`
overrides them, and no planner or drafter may lift either phrase.** No sense, beat or fact was altered in any
    of the eight chapters.
 4. **Two records of the close were wrong about the text.** `workspace/volume-04-close/SUMMARY.md` cited
 Repair 4 by *the chapter's own heading is The Seventeenth* without saying which kind of heading it meant — **the
 chapter title is *Struck Out* and *The Seventeenth* is its second section heading, at line 9, so the citation
 was loose rather than false** — and `state/book-metadata.md` described the word-count method as excluding
   headings* when the figures also exclude the `#` title line. **Both corrected in place.**
5. **Operational, and not the writing phase's to make: `workspace/volume-04-close/` has no `.done`.** The
   close audit is complete and the workflow gates dispatch on that marker, so an audited close can re-run and
   re-touch the four repaired chapters. **`PHASE_SYSTEM.md` says only the controller may mark a phase done, and
   no writing phase was instructed to write it here, so it has deliberately not been created.** Every other
   phase directory in the repository carries the marker. **This is for the controller or the operator.**
6. **Carried, not a defect: the protagonist correction is wholly deferred into Volume 5.** Yuan is the named
   protagonist, has been a voice only and off-panel for two volumes of sixteen, and `outline/ending.md` fixes
   that he takes the fall and is gone before ch 1000. The requirement is now a hard constraint on Volume 5's
   shape — **at least one sustained scene in which he acts, or is acted on, away from her hearing, and it costs
   him something the ending still needs** — and it is written into `state/open-threads.md`,
   `state/current.md` and `workspace/volume-04-close-record/PROMPT.md`. **It is load-bearing on a phase that
   has not started.** Do not let Volume 5 open without it.

## Established facts, Volume 4 Batch 0004 SECOND HALF (ch 231–238) — DRAFTED. THE LAST CHAPTER OF THE VOLUME

### The clock, as drafted

**Ch 231 = days 427–433, the 24th–30th · 232 = 434–437, the 1st–4th · 233 = 438–439, the 5th–6th · 234 =
440, the 7th · 235 = 441–454, the 8th–21st · 236 = 455–461, the 22nd–28th · 237 = 462, the 29th · 238 =
463–470, the 30th and then the 1st–7th.** All reconcile against `((day − 134) mod 30) + 1` and against
**day − 373 across days 374–463, day − 463 from day 464.** **The four pinned stage days (386, 412, 440, 462)
and the pinned attendance day (389) are untouched.**

### What the eight chapters established on the page

- **The price, stated before the word (ch 231), once, in the ordinary voice, with nothing attached to it:**
  **"One day out of the three you are keeping."** The count on the back of the dead weight ticket was
  **four marks, unmoved from the tenth of a month that is now two months back**; one spent on the twenty-second
  for four sentences, **one spent on the twenty-sixth to hear the price**, **two left, one of which is the
  price itself and one of which is the last mark she will ever spend on purpose.** He did not advise her
  either way. **The standing was not used in this chapter.**
- **The Hunter at ch 231**, seven days on high ground, three lines in the notebook and no more: **the course
  closed its ground lawfully and correctly; the person in question was hired on by a sub-contractor outside
  the wire at five against six and has put her hours in his book in order with dates; she is working past
  the last stake on a stretch carrying somebody else's mark and has asked for nothing since except bread.**
  **He bought nothing, threatened nothing, touched her, entered nothing and asked her nothing, and said all
  of that in one breath so that she would not have to spend a morning on it.**
- **Two true books and no run (ch 232).** She squared both. **No hour in both and no hour in neither** —
  the best split a person can have and worth nothing. **A run is one hand in order with dates.** She wrote
  it out on the back of the third dead weight ticket, took eleven minutes a line with the flat of a second
  finger, and **did not ask for the copy she had said out loud once, and the sub-contractor did not offer it
  twice and told her one true unasked thing about the course's book instead.** **The calm is the chapter:**
  she stopped. **She is not calm because anything got better.**
- **The blind run entered (ch 233).** She asked for it out loud at a trestle in daylight on the fifth and
  **nobody persuaded her.** He gave her three true things: **it is nobody's job, it pays two coppers, and it
  does not go in his book.** **Her name said out loud for the third time on that ground.** **He told her in
  the present tense that the stretch is listed** — the layer lists jobs, not men, and that one is on it
  because it is work nobody will take and nobody has finished — **and did not advise her.** She spent the
  standing on her own word and put it down when the crossing was over.
- **Foundation 8 suppressed (ch 234), with no bracketed line in the chapter.** She said the word in a hut and
  it did not go, and **she did not tell him to let it go**, and the base came up the last step while she was
  standing in the dark not saying anything. **She wanted it on the far-side board more than she ever wanted
  nine counties to hear it, and that was the reason, and it is the mistake she understood too late to undo.**
  **On the far side a keeper ruled a line, wrote the date at the left, and did not fill the rest of it.**
- **The clearing (ch 235), fourteen days, the 8th to the 21st.** By hand, by day, alone, with a bar, out at
  about the fifth and in by the seventh, building a hold about forty times an hour and losing about eleven a
  day. **On the fifteenth she went in — down to about the hip, on her back in six inches of it, up again on
  her hands.** Nobody within a day and a half's walk. Nothing broke in her she has not had back once. **On
  the twenty-first she laid the last mark in lime, one mark about the width of a hand, and a second at the
  foot of the rise.**
- **Merit settled on the finished job (ch 235).** He said it in three sentences: the job is on the list, it
  is finished, it has settled; **it is not a man, it never was a man**; and he was taking a share and
  spending it out of her foundation, **out of a base one stage short of finished, because that is the only
  place it is.** **He gave no figure and she did not ask.** The channel came back whole about a quarter of
  an hour later.
- **The right index finger (ch 235), named once.** It went at about the sixth hour at the far end, nine feet
  off a mark laid in lime a quarter of an hour before. **It did not hurt; it went the way the order went out
  of her left hand.** It is the finger that turns a bar over at the end of a stroke and the finger a licence
  nail goes in with, and there is one nailed straight above a bunk nine days west. **She told them out loud
  at the plank, in the ordinary voice, before they could work out whether she was going to lose another one.
  The sub-contractor wrote her down on the twenty-second and did not look up and said *Twenty-two. Five.
  Same as the rest*, and she did not thank him for it.** **Nothing gives it back and nothing offers to.**
- **The honest partial answer (ch 236), once, against a board, in a worker's mouth.** A keeper of about
  fifty-five who has put a strip up for nine years and has never read one of them: the line is not stone and
  was never in the same place on both sides of itself; it is a line of work kept by people going out on it
  with bars; where they go and go on time it holds and where they do not it leaks; what comes out of a
  stretch that has not been kept is drift, thick on the worst-worked ground and thin where nobody works,
  **and his end of it is the thin end because there is nothing this side of the rise for it to come to.**
  **Ninety parts of that is what the men on your side of it say, and I have been out nine years and I have
  never seen the other ten.** **She put it against four months of her own measuring and it did not move, said
  it to herself once in plain words, and built nothing on it and left the tenth part where she keeps the three
  empty dates.**
- **The office above this county (ch 236), in one breath, for the first time in four years: the Standing
  Office of the Second Account.** A middle office with others above it. **He stopped there.** She did not
  ask for one notch more and **wanted to ask whether it is the office that countersigned her petition more
  than she wanted anything else, and did not.**
- **Six weeks (ch 236), said once, in weeks, and the only figure of what was left stated in the batch.**
  He said it unasked: **about six weeks of him, and nothing in this country that makes it longer except a
  base that is not finished yet.**
- **Foundation 9 and the sound past the line (ch 237).** Announced at her own order at the fourth hour on
  the twenty-ninth, in the open, on ground she had tended, and she did not hold it. **Nobody within nine days
  of road heard it.** About a quarter of a second after it went into the ground she felt it go along,
  **east, past the post, out into country she has never been in.** A man of fifty-five at a post stood with
  a pen about an inch above the cloth for as long as it takes to read a line and then wrote, and pushed the
  strip behind the plank, and **did not come and ask her what the sound was.**
- **The claim read aloud (ch 237).** A man at a counter four hundred yards past the post read the conditions
  of a licence out loud to a room, in the ordinary voice, **and the seventh of them was a standing claim of
  eight hundred jin entered on the licence, travelling with it, and not affected by it.** She was nine feet
  outside the door. **Nobody in that room knew she was standing there.** **She did not go in.**
- **The break (ch 238), in this order.** **First the layer:** a dated line went into a board that was already
  on the far side before she got there, in the narrow column, the ninth thing in it since before she came
  east — **he could see the line going in and could not see any part of her on either side of it.** **Then
  her body:** the high ground, then three-quarters of a mile of the low side at four counts with four fingers
  on one hand, a day and a half, one fall on the knee at about a third of the way along, out at the fifth hour
  on the second.
- **Bai Zhu at ch 238.** **He certified the crossing in a Warden's hand, dated on that side of it, signed by
  the office and not by a person** — his second document in the book and the same shape as his first — and
  said in one breath that he had bought nothing, threatened nothing, touched her, entered nothing, asked her
  nothing, **and had not in six days spoken to a man on this side of the post.** **He did not ask her to
  come.**
- **What went into the space at last (ch 238):** **the countersignature on her petition, in an office's
  hand, dated from the far side** — because it is the only thing anybody above him could put there. **It is
  not an answer to his request and his request is still pending.** He said both, in the ordinary voice,
  before she had to ask twice.
- **The final image, exactly as `outline/volume-04.md` fixes it:** a wage-book open on a trestle, a column
  of hours that is hers, **about eleven minutes because of the flat of a second finger and longer because
  the hand on the other side has four fingers' worth of grip left on it**, **the price said out loud in the
  ordinary voice because that is what is said on a line and written down in the price column without
  anybody looking up**, and behind her **a board on a post, dated, nine lines in it, eight of them with
  something on them and one of them with nothing on it at all**, in a hand nobody on the line can read and
  every person on the line can recognise as a hand from the other side. **Nine days of road behind her, a
  floor, nine men, and a tower with a light in it** — said once. **Nothing follows it.**

### Construction budget — spent in ch 231–238, not to reuse

Bracketed layer lines: **one in eight chapters, at ch 237, house form unchanged, and ch 234 carries none by
order.** The seven relicensing conditions: **never re-listed; the seventh read aloud once, in a room, at
ch 237, which is the whole of it.** The suppression arithmetic: **zero — said once at ch 234 in new words
and not argued again.** *There is no form*: zero. *There is no column*: zero. *Nobody asked*: zero in plain
form. *Nobody was thanked*: zero. *Nobody knew*: zero. ***Nobody said anything*: zero** in plain form —
the silence at the pot at ch 235 is *there was a silence at the pot about as long as it takes to count
eleven*. *The record remained*: zero. *Nobody else alive*: zero. **Nine counties is what is heard and four
is what is written, said once in ch 237 and never contradicted.** *The line*, *tending*, *the course* and
*drift*: **used, never explained.** **The honest partial answer is given once, at ch 236, and is ninety
parts.** *Kiln-born*: not on the page. **No new final enemy. Nothing Volume 5 owns is spent. No calendar
month named. No weekday named** — the one that stands, in ch 224, is recorded in `state/current.md` for the
close. ***Eleven weeks*: zero. *About four seconds*: zero.** Banned reflexes: zero. Authorial scaffolding:
zero — a chapter-number leak at ch 231 was caught and cut before the phase finished. **All eight chapters
carry quoted speech and every one of Yuan's sentences is present tense.**

### Volume 4 running ledger — at end of ch 238

| Item | Value at end of ch 238 |
| --- | --- |
| Her stage | **FOUNDATION 9, reached day 462 at her own order, announced in the open where nothing is announced, and the first announcement in four years heard past the line. There is nothing above nine anywhere in the world.** The paper still says Foundation the first stage and she has still never touched it. Short breath four counts everywhere. **The right index finger gone, permanently.** Four fingers' worth of grip left on that hand. The order gone out of the left hand and the heel with it; eleven holds out of about seventy tried. Every county hour is eleven minutes with the flat of a second finger. Right arm open a third of the time. Aperture cracked through and permanent. Not hungry since the first Foundation. Cold the way a stone is cold |
| Where she is | **On the far side of a line of work, in ordinary settled country, at a trestle with a wage-book on it, a fire under it and eleven men on it.** A building with a stove and six men in it. A board on a post. Nobody there has ever heard a stage announced. **Nobody there asked her a question** |
| Licence | **Relicensed on the second set of legs, all three outside this county** (clearance 264, seal 265, countersignature 266). **A document she has never touched.** Seven conditions referenced and never re-listed; the claim the seventh and last. **She did not open it in this volume** |
| In-world date | **Day 470, the seventh of a month** |
| Hours | **On the far side, five coppers a day, in a contractor's book on a trestle, in order, with dates, kept by people who are not a county and are not obliged to keep it.** Before that: two books in two hands past the last stake at five coppers, neither carrying a continuous run, **three empty dates in the second one that are true**, **and two coppers a day more from the fifth for the blind run, paid in her hand and going into no book at all**. **Six counterfoils in the oilcloth, in three marks, none checkable against anything** |
| Settlement | **About six weeks at the last page, said once, in weeks.** Restored at ch 235 out of her foundation by a cleared listed trial. **Merit: one settlement in the whole volume, on a finished job. Nothing in Volume 4 killed anything.** **Ledger: taken twice in Volume 4 and refused once out loud; hers to order at the last page and not ordered** |
| Bounty | **800 jin, standing, the seventh and last condition, travelling on the licence. MOVED and NOT ANSWERED** — read out loud in a room on the far side at ch 237, in the first office within nine days of road that can act on it. The date in Hou Sanliang's counting room is not torn off. **Hou unpaid, patient, never met** |
| The referral | **Up the stair past every office she has ever stood in front of. Its answer space holds a date and the office word *Held*, and now the countersignature on her petition in an office's hand dated from the far side. The request is still pending.** A Wall writ is not issued to a field agent. **The line's own people still do not know she exists** |
| Crew | **nine men and her; ten sleepers in twelve bunks, nine days west. A floor and a tower with a light in it. Said once, at ch 238** |
| Money | **Five coppers a day at the trestle from the fourth, two coppers a day more from the fifth for the blind run, five coppers a day on the far side from the fourth, and bread bought at a store at five coppers a day.** **No balance is stated anywhere in ch 231–238.** **The tin is gone since the evening of day 268** and never counted again |
| The course | **Closed against her, lawfully and correctly, and still closed.** Eleven men and a girl of nineteen stood a stretch for three days for nothing and told her nothing. **They are right and nothing in this volume proves them wrong** |
| The far side | **A trestle with a book, eleven men paid by the day, a room with a counter and a stove, and a post with a nine-year column on it kept by a man of about fifty-five who fills it off a cart and has never read a word of it.** Ordinary country, farmed and lit, and **they asked her nothing** |
| Register / plate | *reconciled* / worn. **She does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop.** The year changed shape and did not close |
| Decision taken | **She is across, entered in a book on the far side in a column that is hers, with a standing of six weeks of him that she has not ordered and a claim of eight hundred jin that has just been read aloud in a room that can act on it. Eighteen years old, four fingers on one hand, a licence she has never opened, and nine days of road behind her** |

## Established facts, Volume 4 Batch 0004 FIRST HALF (ch 223–230) — DRAFTED. EIGHT CHAPTERS OF THE SIXTEEN ARE ON THE PAGE

**This is the standing record for the first half of Batch 0004.** The second half — **ch 231–238, eight

end at ch 230. The cards at `outline/batches/volume-04-batch-0004.md` pin the rest. The batch was split at
the batch midpoint because the first full attempt at sixteen chapters returned without writing anything.

### The clock, as drafted

**Ch 223 opens at day 377, the fourth of a month, and ch 230 ends at day 426, the twenty-third.** Both
reconcile against `((day − 134) mod 30) + 1` and against **day − 373 across days 374 to 463.** Chapter
days as written: **223 = the 4th to the 12th (377–385) · 224 = the 13th (386) · 225 = the 15th to the 20th
(388–393), with the attendance on the sixteenth (389) · 226 = the 21st to the 30th (394–403) · 227 = the
1st to the 8th (404–411) · 228 = the 9th to the 11th (412–414) · 229 = the 12th to the 16th (415–419) ·
230 = the 17th to the 23rd (420–426).**

**A second deliberate deviation from the card table's day row, recorded here so the continuation does not
"correct" it back.** The card row gives ch 225 as 387–393, the 14th to the 20th. **Ch 224 closes at the
seventh hour of the fourteenth, so ch 225 opens on day 388, the fifteenth**, and runs 388–393. Day 387 is
the one day in the volume with no scene in it and it belongs to ch 224's tail; the attendance day (389) is
untouched.

**One deliberate deviation from the card file's day table, recorded here so the continuation does not
"correct" it back.** The card table gives ch 228 as day 412 alone and ch 229 as days 413–419. **Ch 228's
cost is three missing days, so the chapter as written runs 412–414**, and **ch 229 therefore opens on day
415, the twelfth, and covers five days (415–419)** rather than seven. The *effect* on the calendar is nil
and the arc is intact: three empty dates in the sub-contractor's book, eleven men standing a stretch for
nothing, Foundation 7 taken on day 412 exactly as pinned. **The four pinned stage days (386, 412, 440,
462) and the pinned attendance day (389) are untouched.**

### The stages as drafted

**FOUNDATION 5 → FOUNDATION 7 across eight chapters. Two bracketed layer lines, at ch 224 and ch 228, and
six chapters with none.** The suppression at ch 234 has not been written and will carry no bracket.

| Stage | Ch | Day | Bracket | What it cost, on the page, named once |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Foundation 6** | **224** | **386** | **yes** | **The sound in their ears becomes a line on a board, and the people on the line can tell the difference between a stage and a bill.** Eleven idle men look up, the girl of nineteen is the only one who does not, a foreman comes out and looks up and down the run and finds nothing, and a tender puts his hand flat on a board and takes it off again. A board carries dates and stretches and hours and prices and a sound goes into none of them |
| **Foundation 7** | **228** | **412** | **yes** | **Three days she cannot be found, and eleven men who stand a stretch for nothing.** She takes it with her back against a rise three hundred yards past the last stake, in the ordinary way, and the sound arrives on a course nine hundred yards inside the wire and **does not carry a direction** — a board with a hole in it and a hand that does not know where to look |

**No tribulation is drawn in this shape of book. No stage is skipped and none is prepared early.**

### What the eight chapters established on the page

- **The split, shown by the day.** Nine consecutive mornings at the fourth hour into a sub-contractor's
  book with four columns and a painted mark, at five coppers, with bread bought at a store and **no share
  in the cook-fire.** She is **the strongest body on nine days and the slowest hand on a stretch that
  somebody else's mark is painted beside**, and both of those are stated as fact by a foreman and by four
  men and neither is a mystery.
- **The grips (ch 223, 231's material).** The order is gone out of her left hand and every hold is built
  from nothing before the bar lands. **She keeps a count: eleven holds that hold to the end of a stroke
  out of about sixty tried, written on the back of a dead weight ticket with the flat of a second finger
  at eleven minutes a line.** Lost holds are not written down.
- **Cui Bao came out past the wire once, at ch 224**, in the afternoon of the fourteenth, and said the
  whole of it: **the referral was correct, he acted on it inside a week, the run is a fortnight shorter,
  nine men are idle and his daughter is idle, he heard her stage go on the thirteenth and wrote none of
  it down, and he says he would do the whole of it again on any working morning.** *(The line as drafted
  read *on Friday* and was repaired at the Volume 4 close; the weekday is gone.)* He does not want her to
  thank him. **He is
  right and nothing so far in this volume proves him wrong.**
- **MEMBER ABSENT, the third time (ch 225), on the sixteenth.** **One sentence carries it** and the rest
  of the chapter is the reason it is blank. **The word has been offered twice and no man has offered it a
  second time** — the decent man at a trestle inside the wire offered it once and did not offer it again,
  and **the carrier Pei offered it once himself, on an earlier round of his**, and in this scene says
  only that it is not his to fill because it travels down the same road as her hours, and says he will not
  ask her a question about it **because in a year he does not want to be a man who asked one.** **She does
  not say the reason aloud at all — it is given in narration once and not in her mouth: the reason column
  is for hours, and a road put in it is the last thing an office will read about her before it decides
  what she is.** The sheet goes west on the sixteenth and **there is no date west on any board after that
  man for the rest of the season.**
- **The bad flat is described on the ground, as ground and not as a trial (ch 227).** Three-quarters of a
  mile, **no mark, no stake, no board at any point on it.** Drift a foot and a bit on the low side all
  the way. **No ring anywhere in it.** The low side answers every turn of a boot and does not say which
  way it has moved. **Two men in the ground four inches deep with a bar laid across them, drift closed
  over the both of them, and the bar still there.** **Two men and a day and a half to walk it.** **The
  shortage was never strength — it is how long a person can be wrong in it and not go in.** **She went
  down into it on one knee on the first of the month and has not hired onto it and has not asked.**
- **The three missing days (ch 228) are not the bad flat.** They are **the ground in between** — two
  hundred yards past the last mark, on nobody's plank, quiet, and the only ground within that distance
  where the base will turn. **She has not been on the bad flat and no card before ch 233 may put her
  there.** She slept in her own hut, cut a man's better bar out of a lean-to and left the other one.
- **Eleven men and a girl of nineteen stood a stretch for three days, unpaid, and told her nothing** —
  three new lines on the foreman's board, dated the ninth, the tenth and the eleventh, with a stretch in
  the column and **no man against any of them.** She found out from a plank at forty feet. **Being carried
  for three days by people who are right about her is worse than being refused by them**, and that is said
  once and not argued.
- **The three empty dates are entered in the sub-contractor's book and left empty (ch 229).** He squares
  the book, runs his thumb down three empty lines, goes on, and says: **if I put hours in there and a man
  reads that book in six weeks he has got a hole he cannot see, and a hole he cannot see is worse than one
  he can.** **A hole you cannot see is worse than one you can** is the line of the chapter and it is his.
- **A fifth counterfoil (ch 229), in a second contractor's mark.** Four of one mark and now **one in a
  different mark from a man not answerable to the man who struck her name out of a book.** **A mark
  nobody checks is worth what the man who makes it is worth: it goes in a coat, it is carried about by a
  person, and it is kept — and not one of the five can be compared with either book.**
- **Wei Ran, once, at ch 229, and off page.** Twenty coppers and a road, not a favour. **She has taken
  two loads past the cart turn in four years and went round both times, and about half the people who look
  at ground nobody hires on go.** Nothing is a romance.
- **New minors, both closed in their own chapter.** **Pei**, a carrier of about sixty, the hand that
  writes the attendance column (ch 225). **The woman at the store**, who has thought about the fact that
  there is a woman coming out of that line without a pot, and has not asked (ch 226).
- **The decision (ch 230), taken in her own head and not spoken.** Two books cannot make a run. Hours in
  order with dates do not cross a boundary no county paper crosses. A listed trial is not paid by hours in
  either book. **She decides to hold the Ledger for the crossing and not for a stage, chooses the day —
  the fifth of the next month, thirteen days off, inside the turn of the base — and says neither the word
  nor the price.** He is offered the chance and takes it: *"Say it and I hold it as long as you want it
  held."* · *"Not for a stage."* · *"No."* · *"Not today."* · *"You know the day."* **Ledger is not
  ordered in ch 223–230.**
- **The fear is admitted once (ch 230).** Not of the bad flat. Of **spending him on purpose, out of a base
  that is one stage short of finished**, with nothing at the end of it this time but a quarter of a mile of
  ground nobody records. **She went and looked at it and put it down and picked it up about four times,
  and it is not a reason.**
- **The year of non-forgiveness was touched once, at low volume, at ch 230** — she goes on using him on
  purpose, **it is not forgiveness, and the account still carries and will carry at the end of the
  book.** She does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop.

### Construction budget — spent in ch 223–230, not to reuse

Bracketed layer lines: **two in eight chapters, at ch 224 and ch 228**, house form unchanged. **Six
chapters carry none at all.** The seven relicensing conditions re-listed: **zero** — the licence and the
claim are referenced and never told, and the only new object on the licence this half is the Clay Plate as
the thing she has not taken off in four years. The suppression arithmetic: **zero.** ***There is no form*:
zero. *There is no column*: zero** — and this half is full of columns that exist and are full, and ch 229
is about a column with three honest gaps in it. ***The record remained*: zero.*** ***Nobody asked*: zero
in plain form** — the territory is reached through *the word that was not offered a second time* (225)
and *he does not want to be a man who asked one* (225), each once, and they are different constructions.
***Nobody was thanked*: zero.*** ***Nobody knew*: zero.*** ***Nobody said anything*: zero** in plain form.
***Nobody else alive*: zero.*** *The line*, *tending*, *the course* and *drift* are **spent words from
ch 199 and are used and never re-explained** — and the honest partial answer to what the drift is made of
is **NOT given in ch 223–230**; that is ch 236. **No office named. No new final enemy. Nothing Volume 5
owns is spent.** *The shape of a happy life*: zero. ***Kiln-born* and the eleven designations: **not on
the page.** **The right index finger: named nowhere, priced nowhere, foreshadowed nowhere** — one draft
line at ch 230 that came close was cut before the chapter was finished. Weekdays: **zero named.** No
calendar month named anywhere in ch 223–230. ***Eleven weeks*: zero. *About four seconds*: zero** — one
draft instance at ch 227 was cut. **Banned reflexes: zero.** **Authorial scaffolding: zero** — two draft
leaks, a chapter number at ch 223 and a batch phrase at ch 225, were both cut before the phase finished.
**Every chapter carries quoted speech**, and **every one of Yuan's sentences is present tense.**

### Carried forward, unchanged, into ch 231–238

- **FOUNDATION 7.** Ceiling on the front of the relicensed licence **still reads Foundation the first
  stage** and she has still never touched it. **Foundation 8 at ch 234 (day 440, SUPPRESSED, no bracketed
  line in the chapter) · Foundation 9 at ch 237 (day 462, the announcement that goes past the line).**
  **Ch 236 is the first page on which Yuan reads the office name above this county, and he has said four
  times that he cannot.**
- **Short breath four counts everywhere, three is gone. The order is gone out of the left hand and the
  heel with it. The writing hand is unchanged — eleven minutes with the flat of a second finger. Eleven
  holds that hold, out of about sixty tried.** The **right index finger is whole and unspent.**
- **MEMBER ABSENT, three times, permanent**, with the reason column a foot and a half wide and blank, and
  **the sheet has gone west for the last time this season.**
- **The claim stands with its date.** Hou Sanliang unpaid, patient and never met. **The request is still
  pending and the space has not been answered in any form.** The referral is still pending and executable
  only past the boundary.
- **The course is closed against her, lawfully and correctly, and stays closed.** Two books in two hands,
  neither carrying a continuous run, **and three empty dates in the second one that are true.**
- **Five counterfoils in the oilcloth, in two marks, none comparable with either book. The Clay Plate is
  under them against her chest. Three dead weight tickets, two of them full, one carrying the ration's
  four marks.**
- **The blind run is not entered.** It has been seen from a rise, walked with a cart and entered on one
  knee, and the sub-contractor has named it twice and warned her about it once.
- **Ledger is hers to order and is NOT ordered. The decision is taken; the word is not said and the price
  is not said. The channel has four marks on it and no figure of what is left is stated anywhere.**
- **Merit: none. Nothing kills anything and nothing clears a listed trial.**
- **Wei Ran is off page.** Pei and the woman at the store are closed in their own chapters. **The Hunter
  is a man in a plain coat at two hundred yards on the twenty-third, not writing, and he is named
  nowhere.** The office above this county is unreadable and unasked for. The far-side board is untouched.
- **The register still reads *reconciled*. The Clay Plate still worn. Shen Xiu unforgiven and unthanked.
  The three sponsors silent. What became of the eleven is not raised. Ning Wu does not appear. The crew
  at Qinghe is nine days west and is not mentioned.** **She does not forgive him and she does not tell him
  to stop.**

The heel's loss is not the cost; every county hour is still eleven minutes with the flat of a second finger. Right arm open a third of the time. Aperture cracked through and permanent. ## Established facts, Volume 4 Batch 0004 (ch 223–238) — THE PLANNING RECORD (SUPERSEDED: all sixteen chapters are now on the page)

**Nothing in this block is on the page.** It records what sixteen cards at
`outline/batches/volume-04-batch-0004.md` pin, so that no drafting phase could move one. **It was written
before either half of the batch was drafted and both halves are now on the page**; what is on the page is
recorded in the FIRST HALF, SECOND HALF and *Volume 4 complete* blocks above, and those govern. Read this
block for the pins only. **Ch 223–230
ARE NOW DRAFTED and are recorded in the block above; this block governs ch 231–238 only, and the position
those eight chapters start from is the ch 230 ledger above, not the ch 222 one.** Batch 0004 is sixteen
chapters, not fourteen — 223–238 inclusive, per the allocation table in `outline/volume-04.md`
*Pressure rotation across the volume*.

- **The clock.** Ch 223 opens at about **day 377** and the batch runs to about **day 470**. **Months are thirty
  days and the month-day of any day is `((day − 134) mod 30) + 1`**; days 374–463 read day − 373 and day 464
  begins the next month. **The old figure `day − 133` was Volume 3's offset and is wrong here.** Day 376 is the
  third, as `chapter-0222.md` says.
- **The four stages, pinned and unmovable.** **Foundation 6 at ch 224 (day 386, bracket) · Foundation 7 at ch 228
  (day 412, bracket) · Foundation 8 at ch 234 (day 440, SUPPRESSED at her own order, and its chapter carries no
  bracketed line at all) · Foundation 9 at ch 237 (day 462, bracket past the line).** Intervals twenty-six,
  twenty-six, twenty-eight, twenty-two. **Three bracketed lines in sixteen chapters, at 224, 228 and 237, and
  thirteen chapters carry none.**
- **The attendance.** **Day 389, the sixteenth**, thirty days on from day 359, **one sentence inside ch 225**, and
  the last one before the volume closes. MEMBER ABSENT goes with it a third time, permanent, reason column blank.
- **What the batch closes.** The blind run hired onto and cleared by hand (ch 233–235) · the listed trial cleared
  and **Merit settling on a finished job** · the channel restored out of it, he intercepts a share and spends it
  out of her foundation · **the right index finger gone permanently at ch 235 and offered back by nobody** · the
  announcement past the line at ch 237 · the course closed lawfully against her · the break at ch 238 · the volume's
  fixed final image. **Nothing kills anybody; Volume 3's kill is spent.**
- **Ledger.** Held once, briefly, at ch 233, at a price stated before the word, put down when it is over, and hers
  to order at the last page. The standing is not taught again.
- **Still unresolved at ch 238 and not to be answered here.** The Hunter's request · the office above this county,
  which **he reads at ch 236 and no page earlier** · the dated entry against the claim · what became of the eleven
  · the Scribe, the Anchor and Yuan's origin.
- **Two items this batch may not settle, carried to the volume close.** **The point of view** — Yuan is a voice
  only in Volume 4 and ch 179–238 is close third on Ning Yan; see `state/open-threads.md`. And **the volume-close
  phase itself**, which the drafting phase must write as `workspace/volume-04-close/PROMPT.md` in the same call as
  ch 238.
